    34→  constructor(

    52→
    53→  async login(loginDto: LoginDto): Promise<LoginServiceResult> {
    54→    // 1. Verify CipherBox-issued JWT.
    55→    // All auth methods now go through: CipherBox identity provider -> Core Kit loginWithJWT -> /auth/login.
    56→    const payload = await this.verifyCipherBoxJwt(loginDto.idToken);
    57→
    58→    // 2. Find or create user
    59→    let user = await this.userRepository.findOne({
    60→      where: { publicKey: loginDto.publicKey },
    61→    });
    62→
    63→    // 2b. Placeholder publicKey resolution for Core Kit identity provider.
    64→    //
    65→    // Two scenarios need resolution:
    66→    //
    67→    // A) FIRST LOGIN completion: User was created with a placeholder publicKey
    68→    //    ('pending-core-kit-{verifierId}') during identity token issuance.
    69→    //    After Core Kit loginWithJWT, the client calls /auth/login with the
    70→    //    REAL publicKey. Find the placeholder user and update their publicKey.
    71→    //
    72→    // B) REQUIRED_SHARE temp auth: MFA is enabled but the new device can't
    73→    //    reconstruct the Core Kit key (missing device factor). The client
    74→    //    sends a placeholder publicKey to get a temp access token for the
    75→    //    device approval bulletin board. The user already has a REAL publicKey
    76→    //    from their first login, so we look them up by identity (authMethod
    77→    //    identifierHash) instead of by publicKey.
    78→    if (!user) {
    79→      const verifierId = payload.verifierId || payload.sub;
    80→
    81→      if (verifierId) {
    82→        // A) Check for user with placeholder publicKey (first login completion)
    83→        const placeholderUser = await this.userRepository.findOne({
    84→          where: { publicKey: `pending-core-kit-${verifierId}` },
    85→        });
    86→        if (placeholderUser) {
    87→          this.logger.log(`Resolving placeholder publicKey for user ${placeholderUser.id}`);
    88→          // Only update publicKey if the incoming key is a real one (not another placeholder)
    89→          if (!loginDto.publicKey.startsWith('pending-core-kit-')) {
    90→            placeholderUser.publicKey = loginDto.publicKey;
    91→            user = await this.userRepository.save(placeholderUser);
    92→          } else {
    93→            user = placeholderUser;
    94→          }
    95→        }
    96→      }
    97→
    98→      // B) REQUIRED_SHARE temp auth: user already completed first login (has real
    99→      //    publicKey), so no placeholder row exists. Look up directly by userId
   100→      //    from the JWT sub — works for all auth method types (email, Google, wallet).
   101→      if (!user && payload.sub && loginDto.publicKey.startsWith('pending-core-kit-')) {
   102→        const existingUser = await this.userRepository.findOne({
   103→          where: { id: payload.sub },
   104→        });
   105→        if (existingUser) {
   106→          this.logger.log(
   107→            `REQUIRED_SHARE temp auth: found existing user ${existingUser.id} by userId`
   108→          );
   109→          user = existingUser;
   110→        }
   111→      }
   112→    }
   113→
   114→    const isNewUser = !user;
   115→    if (!user) {
   116→      user = await this.userRepository.save({
   117→        publicKey: loginDto.publicKey,
   118→      });
   119→    }
   120→
   121→    // 3. Find or create auth method
   122→    // The identity controller already created the auth method
   123→    // (with the correct type: 'google', 'email', or 'wallet'). Look up by
   124→    // userId + identifierHash to avoid creating duplicates with a hardcoded type.
   125→    let authMethod: AuthMethod | null;
   126→
   127→    const identifier = payload.email || payload.sub;
   128→    if (!identifier) {
   129→      this.logger.warn('CipherBox JWT missing both email and sub claims');
   130→      throw new UnauthorizedException('Invalid identity token: missing identifier');
   131→    }
   132→    const identifierHash = this.siweService.hashIdentifier(identifier);
   133→    authMethod = await this.authMethodRepository.findOne({
   134→      where: { userId: user.id, identifierHash },
   135→    });
   136→    if (!authMethod) {
   137→      // Fallback: find any auth method for this user (identity controller should have created one)
   138→      authMethod = await this.authMethodRepository.findOne({
   139→        where: { userId: user.id },
   140→      });
   141→    }
   142→    if (!authMethod) {
   143→      // Safety net: create auth method if identity controller didn't (shouldn't happen in practice)
   144→      // All logins go through loginType='corekit' now, but infer type from identifier format
   145→      const inferredType = identifier.startsWith('0x') ? 'wallet' : 'email';
   146→      const display =
   147→        inferredType === 'wallet'
   148→          ? this.siweService.truncateWalletAddress(identifier)
   149→          : this.siweService.truncateEmail(identifier);
   150→      authMethod = await this.authMethodRepository.save({
   151→        userId: user.id,
   152→        type: inferredType,
   153→        identifier: identifierHash,
   154→        identifierHash,
   155→        identifierDisplay: display,
   156→      });
   157→    }
   158→
   159→    // 4. Update last used timestamp
   160→    authMethod.lastUsedAt = new Date();
   161→    await this.authMethodRepository.save(authMethod);
   162→
   163→    // 5. Create tokens
   164→    // REQUIRED_SHARE temp auth: issue scoped, non-refreshable tokens that only
   165→    // grant access to the device-approval bulletin board. The new device will
   166→    // do a second /auth/login with the real publicKey after key reconstruction.
   167→    const isTempAuth = loginDto.publicKey.startsWith('pending-core-kit-') && !isNewUser;
   168→    const tokens = await this.tokenService.createTokens(
   169→      user.id,
   170→      user.publicKey,
   171→      isTempAuth ? { scope: ['device-approval'], skipRefreshToken: true } : undefined
   172→    );
   173→
   174→    return {
   175→      accessToken: tokens.accessToken,
   176→      refreshToken: tokens.refreshToken,
   177→      isNewUser,
   178→    };
   179→  }
   180→
   181→  /**
   182→   * Verify a CipherBox-issued JWT for Core Kit login flow.
   183→   * Since we are the identity provider, we verify against our own JWKS.
   184→   */
   185→  private async verifyCipherBoxJwt(
   186→    idToken: string
   187→  ): Promise<{ sub?: string; verifierId?: string; email?: string }> {
   188→    try {
   189→      const jwksData = this.jwtIssuerService.getJwksData();
   190→      const jwks = jose.createLocalJWKSet(jwksData);
   191→      const { payload } = await jose.jwtVerify(idToken, jwks, {
   192→        issuer: 'cipherbox',
   193→        audience: 'web3auth',
   194→        algorithms: ['RS256'],
   195→      });
   196→      return {
   197→        sub: payload.sub,
   198→        verifierId: payload.sub,
   199→        email: payload.email as string | undefined,
   200→      };
   201→    } catch (error) {
   202→      this.logger.warn(
   203→        `CipherBox JWT verification failed: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'unknown'}`
   204→      );
   205→      throw new UnauthorizedException('Invalid CipherBox identity token');
   206→    }
   207→  }
   208→
   209→  async refresh(
   210→    refreshToken: string,
   211→    userId: string,
   212→    publicKey: string
   213→  ): Promise<RefreshServiceResult> {
   214→    const tokens = await this.tokenService.rotateRefreshToken(refreshToken, userId, publicKey);
   215→
   216→    return {
   217→      accessToken: tokens.accessToken,
   218→      refreshToken: tokens.refreshToken,
   219→    };
   220→  }
   221→
   222→  async logout(userId: string): Promise<LogoutResponseDto> {
   223→    await this.tokenService.revokeAllUserTokens(userId);
   224→    return { success: true };
   225→  }
   226→
   227→  /**
   228→   * Permanently delete a user account and all associated data.
   229→   *
   230→   * 1. Unpins all IPFS content from the local Kubo node (best-effort)
   231→   * 2. Deletes the user row — ON DELETE CASCADE handles cleanup of
   232→   *    auth_methods, refresh_tokens, vaults, pinned_cids, folder_ipns,
   233→   *    ipns_republish_schedule, shares, share_keys, and share_invites.
   234→   */
   235→  async deleteAccount(userId: string): Promise<{ success: boolean }> {
   236→    // Fetch all pinned CIDs before cascade deletes the records
   237→    const pinnedCids = await this.pinnedCidRepository.find({
   238→      where: { userId },
   239→      select: ['cid'],
   240→    });
   241→
   242→    // Unpin all content from Kubo (best-effort, don't block deletion on failure)
   243→    if (pinnedCids.length > 0) {
   244→      const results = await Promise.allSettled(
   245→        pinnedCids.map((pin) => this.ipfsProvider.unpinFile(pin.cid))
   246→      );
   247→      const failed = results.filter((r) => r.status === 'rejected').length;
   248→      this.logger.log(
   249→        `Unpinned ${pinnedCids.length - failed}/${pinnedCids.length} CIDs for user ${userId}` +
   250→          (failed > 0 ? ` (${failed} failed)` : '')
   251→      );
   252→    }
   253→
   254→    // Delete user row — cascade handles all related DB records
   255→    const result = await this.userRepository.delete(userId);
   256→    if (result.affected === 0) {
   257→      throw new BadRequestException('Account not found');
   258→    }
   259→    this.logger.log(`Account deleted: userId=${userId}`);
   260→    return { success: true };
   261→  }
   262→
   263→  /**
   264→   * Refresh tokens by searching for the matching refresh token across all users.
   265→   * This allows refresh without requiring the (possibly expired) access token.
   266→   */
   267→  async refreshByToken(refreshToken: string): Promise<RefreshServiceResult> {
   268→    // Find candidate tokens by prefix for O(1) lookup instead of O(N) Argon2 scan
   269→    const prefix = refreshToken.substring(0, 16);
   270→    const tokens = await this.refreshTokenRepository.find({
   271→      where: {
   272→        tokenPrefix: prefix,
   273→        revokedAt: IsNull(),
   274→      },
   275→      relations: ['user'],
   276→    });
   277→
   278→    // Find matching token by verifying against hashes
   279→    let validToken: RefreshToken | null = null;
   280→    for (const token of tokens) {
   281→      // Skip expired tokens
   282→      if (token.expiresAt < new Date()) {
   283→        continue;
   284→      }
   285→      try {
   286→        if (await argon2.verify(token.tokenHash, refreshToken)) {
   287→          validToken = token;
   288→          break;
   289→        }
   290→      } catch {
   291→        // argon2.verify throws on invalid hash format, continue checking
   292→        continue;
   293→      }
   294→    }
   295→
   296→    if (!validToken) {
   297→      throw new UnauthorizedException('Invalid or expired refresh token');
   298→    }
   299→
   300→    // Revoke old token
   301→    validToken.revokedAt = new Date();
   302→    await this.refreshTokenRepository.save(validToken);
   303→
   304→    // Create new tokens
   305→    const newTokens = await this.tokenService.createTokens(
   306→      validToken.userId,
   307→      validToken.user.publicKey
   308→    );
   309→
   310→    // Look up user's email from their most recently used auth method
   311→    // (covers both email and google auth methods)
   312→    const emailMethod = await this.authMethodRepository.findOne({
   313→      where: [
   314→        { userId: validToken.userId, type: 'email' },
   315→        { userId: validToken.userId, type: 'google' },
   316→      ],
   317→      order: { lastUsedAt: 'DESC' },
   318→    });
   319→
   320→    return {
   321→      accessToken: newTokens.accessToken,
   322→      refreshToken: newTokens.refreshToken,
   323→      // H-09: Fall back to undefined instead of leaking raw identifier hash
   324→      email: emailMethod?.identifierDisplay || undefined,
   325→    };
   326→  }
   327→
   328→  /**
   329→   * Get all linked auth methods for a user.
   330→   * Returns identifierDisplay (human-readable) for all method types.
   331→   * Falls back to identifier if identifierDisplay is not set.
   332→   */
   333→  async getLinkedMethods(userId: string): Promise<AuthMethodResponseDto[]> {
   334→    const methods = await this.authMethodRepository.find({
   335→      where: { userId },
   336→      order: { createdAt: 'ASC' },
   337→    });
   338→
   339→    return methods.map((method) => ({
   340→      id: method.id,
   341→      type: method.type,
   342→      // H-09: Fall back to '[redacted]' instead of leaking raw identifier hash
   343→      identifier: method.identifierDisplay || '[redacted]',
   344→      lastUsedAt: method.lastUsedAt,
   345→      createdAt: method.createdAt,
   346→    }));
   347→  }
   348→
   349→  /**
   350→   * Link a new auth method to an existing user account.
   351→   *
   352→   * For Google/email: verifies CipherBox-issued JWT to confirm ownership of the new method.
   353→   * For wallet: verifies SIWE message+signature to confirm wallet ownership.
   354→   *
   355→   * Cross-account collision: if the auth method already belongs to a different user,
   356→   * a BadRequestException is thrown (user must unlink from the other account first).
   357→   */
   358→  async linkMethod(userId: string, linkDto: LinkMethodDto): Promise<AuthMethodResponseDto[]> {
   359→    // 1. Get the user
   360→    const user = await this.userRepository.findOne({ where: { id: userId } });
   361→    if (!user) {
   362→      throw new UnauthorizedException('User not found');
   363→    }
   364→
   365→    const authMethodType = linkDto.loginType;
   366→
   367→    if (authMethodType === 'wallet') {
   368→      // Wallet linking: verify SIWE message + signature
   369→      return this.linkWalletMethod(userId, linkDto);
   370→    }
   371→
   372→    // Google/email linking: verify CipherBox-issued JWT
   373→    return this.linkJwtMethod(userId, linkDto);
   374→  }
   375→
   376→  /**
   377→   * Link a Google or email auth method via CipherBox JWT verification.
   378→   */
   379→  private async linkJwtMethod(
   380→    userId: string,
   381→    linkDto: LinkMethodDto
   382→  ): Promise<AuthMethodResponseDto[]> {
   383→    // 1. Verify the CipherBox-issued JWT
   384→    const payload = await this.verifyCipherBoxJwt(linkDto.idToken);
   385→
   386→    // 2. Determine type and identifier, hash for lookup
   387→    const authMethodType = linkDto.loginType;
   388→    const identifier = payload.email || payload.sub;
   389→    if (!identifier) {
   390→      throw new BadRequestException('Cannot determine identifier from JWT');
   391→    }
   392→    const identifierHash = this.siweService.hashIdentifier(identifier);
   393→
   394→    // 3. Check cross-account collision: same identifier linked to a different user
   395→    const crossAccountMethod = await this.authMethodRepository.findOne({
   396→      where: {
   397→        type: authMethodType,
   398→        identifierHash,
   399→        userId: Not(userId),
   400→      },
   401→    });
   402→
   403→    if (crossAccountMethod) {
   404→      throw new BadRequestException(
   405→        `This ${authMethodType === 'google' ? 'Google account' : 'email'} is already linked to another account`
   406→      );
   407→    }
   408→
   409→    // 4. Check if this exact method is already linked to this user
   410→    const existingMethod = await this.authMethodRepository.findOne({
   411→      where: {
   412→        userId,
   413→        type: authMethodType,
   414→        identifierHash,
   415→      },
   416→    });
   417→
   418→    if (existingMethod) {
   419→      throw new BadRequestException('This auth method is already linked to your account');
   420→    }
   421→
   422→    // 5. Create new AuthMethod entity with hashed identifier
   423→    await this.authMethodRepository.save({
   424→      userId,
   425→      type: authMethodType,
   426→      identifier: identifierHash,
   427→      identifierHash,
   428→      identifierDisplay: payload.email ? this.siweService.truncateEmail(payload.email) : identifier,
   429→      lastUsedAt: new Date(),
   430→    });
   431→
   432→    // 6. Return updated list of methods
   433→    return this.getLinkedMethods(userId);
   434→  }
   435→
   436→  /**
   437→   * Link a wallet auth method via SIWE verification.
   438→   */
   439→  private async linkWalletMethod(
   440→    userId: string,
   441→    linkDto: LinkMethodDto
   442→  ): Promise<AuthMethodResponseDto[]> {
   443→    // 1. Validate required SIWE fields
   444→    if (!linkDto.walletAddress || !linkDto.siweMessage || !linkDto.siweSignature) {
   445→      throw new BadRequestException(
   446→        'walletAddress, siweMessage, and siweSignature are required for wallet linking'
   447→      );
   448→    }
   449→
   450→    // 2. Verify SIWE signature with nonce consumption (C-01: prevent replay)
   451→    const parsed = parseSiweMessage(linkDto.siweMessage);
   452→    if (!parsed.nonce) {
   453→      throw new BadRequestException('Invalid SIWE message: missing nonce');
   454→    }
   455→
   456→    // Consume nonce from Redis (single-use) — same pattern as identity.controller.ts walletLogin
   457→    const nonceKey = `siwe:nonce:${parsed.nonce}`;
   458→    let nonceDeleted: number;
   459→    try {
   460→      nonceDeleted = await this.redis.del(nonceKey);
   461→    } catch (err) {
   462→      this.logger.error('Redis error during nonce consumption', err);
   463→      throw new BadRequestException('Nonce verification failed');
   464→    }
   465→    if (!nonceDeleted) {
   466→      throw new BadRequestException('Invalid or expired nonce');
   467→    }
   468→
   469→    const walletAddress = await this.siweService.verifySiweMessage(
   470→      linkDto.siweMessage,
   471→      linkDto.siweSignature as `0x${string}`,
   472→      parsed.nonce
   473→    );
   474→
   475→    // 3. Hash the wallet address for lookup
   476→    const addressHash = this.siweService.hashWalletAddress(walletAddress);
   477→
   478→    // 4. Check cross-account collision: same wallet linked to a different user
   479→    const crossAccountMethod = await this.authMethodRepository.findOne({
   480→      where: {
   481→        type: 'wallet',
   482→        identifierHash: addressHash,
   483→        userId: Not(userId),
   484→      },
   485→    });
   486→
   487→    if (crossAccountMethod) {
   488→      throw new BadRequestException('This wallet is already linked to another account');
   489→    }
   490→
   491→    // 5. Check if this wallet is already linked to this user
   492→    const existingMethod = await this.authMethodRepository.findOne({
   493→      where: {
   494→        userId,
   495→        type: 'wallet',
   496→        identifierHash: addressHash,
   497→      },
   498→    });
   499→
   500→    if (existingMethod) {
   501→      throw new BadRequestException('This wallet is already linked to your account');
   502→    }
   503→
   504→    // 6. Create wallet auth method with hash + truncated display
   505→    const truncated = this.siweService.truncateWalletAddress(walletAddress);
   506→    await this.authMethodRepository.save({
   507→      userId,
   508→      type: 'wallet',
   509→      identifier: addressHash,
   510→      identifierHash: addressHash,
   511→      identifierDisplay: truncated,
   512→      lastUsedAt: new Date(),
   513→    });
   514→
   515→    // 7. Return updated list of methods
   516→    return this.getLinkedMethods(userId);
   517→  }
   518→
   519→  /**
   520→   * Unlink an auth method from a user account.
   521→   * Cannot unlink the last remaining auth method.
   522→   */
   523→  async unlinkMethod(userId: string, methodId: string): Promise<void> {
   524→    await this.authMethodRepository.manager.transaction(async (manager) => {
   525→      // 1. Lock all auth methods for this user to prevent concurrent unlinks
   526→      const methods = await manager
   527→        .createQueryBuilder(AuthMethod, 'am')
   528→        .setLock('pessimistic_write')
   529→        .where('am.userId = :userId', { userId })
   530→        .getMany();
   531→
   532→      // 2. Find the target method
   533→      const method = methods.find((m) => m.id === methodId);
   534→      if (!method) {
   535→        throw new BadRequestException('Auth method not found');
   536→      }
   537→
   538→      // 3. Cannot unlink if only 1 method remains
   539→      if (methods.length <= 1) {
   540→        throw new BadRequestException('Cannot unlink your last auth method');
   541→      }
   542→
   543→      // 4. Delete the method
   544→      await manager.remove(method);
   545→    });
   546→  }
   547→
   548→  /**
   549→   * Test-only login that bypasses Core Kit entirely.
   550→   * Guarded by TEST_LOGIN_SECRET env var — never available in production.
   551→   *
   552→   * Creates/finds user by email, generates a deterministic secp256k1 keypair,
   553→   * and issues tokens. Returns the keypair so E2E tests can initialize vaults.
   554→   */
   555→  async testLogin(
   556→    email: string,
   557→    secret: string
   558→  ): Promise<{
   559→    accessToken: string;
   560→    refreshToken: string;
   561→    isNewUser: boolean;
   562→    publicKeyHex: string;
   563→    privateKeyHex: string;
   564→  }> {
   565→    // 1. Defense-in-depth: never allow in production regardless of env var
   566→    const nodeEnv = this.configService.get<string>('NODE_ENV');
   567→    if (nodeEnv === 'production') {
   568→      throw new ForbiddenException('Test login is not available in production');
   569→    }
   570→
   571→    // 2. Validate TEST_LOGIN_SECRET with timing-safe comparison
   572→    const expectedSecret = this.configService.get<string>('TEST_LOGIN_SECRET');
   573→    if (!expectedSecret) {
   574→      throw new ForbiddenException('Test login is not enabled');
   575→    }
   576→    const secretBuf = Buffer.from(secret);
   577→    const expectedBuf = Buffer.from(expectedSecret);
   578→    if (secretBuf.length !== expectedBuf.length || !timingSafeEqual(secretBuf, expectedBuf)) {
   579→      throw new UnauthorizedException('Invalid test login secret');
   580→    }
   581→
   582→    // 2. Generate deterministic secp256k1 keypair from email
   583→    const { publicKeyHex, privateKeyHex } = this.generateDeterministicKeypair(email);
   584→
   585→    // 3. Find or create user by email (hash-based lookup)
   586→    const normalizedEmail = email.toLowerCase().trim();
   587→    const identifierHash = this.siweService.hashIdentifier(normalizedEmail);
   588→
   589→    const existingMethod = await this.authMethodRepository.findOne({
   590→      where: { type: 'email', identifierHash },
   591→      relations: ['user'],
   592→    });
   593→
   594→    let user: User;
   595→    let isNewUser = false;
   596→
   597→    if (existingMethod) {
   598→      user = existingMethod.user;
   599→      // Update publicKey to match deterministic keypair (may differ from Core Kit key)
   600→      if (user.publicKey !== publicKeyHex) {
   601→        user.publicKey = publicKeyHex;
   602→        await this.userRepository.save(user);
   603→      }
   604→      existingMethod.lastUsedAt = new Date();
   605→      await this.authMethodRepository.save(existingMethod);
   606→    } else {
   607→      isNewUser = true;
   608→      user = await this.userRepository.save({ publicKey: publicKeyHex });
   609→      await this.authMethodRepository.save({
   610→        userId: user.id,
   611→        type: 'email',
   612→        identifier: identifierHash,
   613→        identifierHash,
   614→        identifierDisplay: this.siweService.truncateEmail(normalizedEmail),
   615→        lastUsedAt: new Date(),
   616→      });
   617→    }
   618→
   619→    // 4. Issue tokens
   620→    const tokens = await this.tokenService.createTokens(user.id, user.publicKey);
   621→
   622→    this.logger.log(`Test login: userId=${user.id}, isNew=${isNewUser}`);
   623→
   624→    return {
   625→      accessToken: tokens.accessToken,
   626→      refreshToken: tokens.refreshToken,
   627→      isNewUser,
   628→      publicKeyHex,
   629→      privateKeyHex,
   630→    };
   631→  }
   632→
   633→  /**
   634→   * Generate a deterministic secp256k1 keypair from an email address.
   635→   * Same email always produces the same keypair, enabling consistent
   636→   * vault encryption across test runs.
   637→   */
   638→  private generateDeterministicKeypair(email: string): {
   639→    publicKeyHex: string;
   640→    privateKeyHex: string;
   641→  } {
   642→    // Derive 32-byte private key from email via SHA-256
   643→    const seed = createHash('sha256')
   644→      .update(`cipherbox-test-keypair:${email.toLowerCase().trim()}`)
   645→      .digest();
   646→
   647→    // Ensure the seed is a valid secp256k1 private key (must be in [1, n-1])
   648→    const n = BigInt('0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFEBAAEDCE6AF48A03BBFD25E8CD0364141');
   649→    let keyInt = BigInt('0x' + seed.toString('hex'));
   650→    keyInt = (keyInt % (n - 1n)) + 1n;
   651→    const privateKeyHex = keyInt.toString(16).padStart(64, '0');
   652→
   653→    const ecdh = createECDH('secp256k1');
   654→    ecdh.setPrivateKey(Buffer.from(privateKeyHex, 'hex'));
   655→    const publicKeyHex = ecdh.getPublicKey().toString('hex'); // 65 bytes uncompressed
   656→
   657→    return { publicKeyHex, privateKeyHex };
   658→  }
   659→}
   660→

    44→  async createShare(sharerId: string, dto: CreateShareDto): Promise<Share> {

   368→  async getInviteStatus(token: string): Promise<{ status: string } | null> {

   370→

   374→    if (invite.status === 'active' && invite.expiresAt < new Date()) {

   386→   */
   387→  async getInviteForClaim(token: string): Promise<ShareInvite | null> {
   388→    const invite = await this.inviteRepo.findOne({ where: { token } });
   389→
   390→    if (!invite) return null;
   391→
   392→    // Auto-expire: delete and return null if past expiry
   393→    if (invite.status === 'active' && invite.expiresAt < new Date()) {
   394→      await this.inviteRepo.remove(invite);
   395→      return null;
   396→    }
   397→
   398→    // Only return active invites for the claim flow
   399→    if (invite.status !== 'active') return null;
   400→
   401→    return invite;
   402→  }
   403→
   404→  /**
   405→   * Claim an invite: atomic single-claim with Share + ShareKey creation.
   406→   *
   407→   * Uses UPDATE ... WHERE to prevent race conditions on concurrent claims.
   408→   * Self-claim prevention: sharer cannot claim their own invite.
   409→   * Creates standard Phase 14 Share + ShareKey records from re-wrapped keys.
   410→   */
   411→  async claimInvite(
   412→    token: string,
   413→    claimerId: string,
   414→    dto: ClaimInviteDto
   415→  ): Promise<{ shareId: string }> {
   416→    // First, look up the invite to get sharer info
   417→    const invite = await this.inviteRepo.findOne({ where: { token } });
   418→
   419→    if (!invite) {
   420→      throw new NotFoundException('Invite not found or expired');
   421→    }
   422→
   423→    // Pre-transaction expiry / status check so expired/revoked invites
   424→    // return 404 instead of leaking through to the atomic UPDATE (which
   425→    // would throw 409 and signal that the token exists).
   426→    if (invite.expiresAt < new Date()) {
   427→      if (invite.status === 'active') {
   428→        await this.inviteRepo.remove(invite);
   429→      }
   430→      throw new NotFoundException('Invite not found or expired');
   431→    }
   432→
   433→    if (invite.status !== 'active') {
   434→      throw new NotFoundException('Invite not found or expired');
   435→    }
   436→
   437→    // Self-claim prevention
   438→    if (invite.sharerId === claimerId) {
   439→      throw new ConflictException('Cannot claim your own invite');
   440→    }
   441→
   442→    // Run atomic claim + Share creation inside a transaction so that
   443→    // a failure after marking the invite as claimed is rolled back.
   444→    return this.dataSource.transaction(async (manager) => {
   445→      // Atomic UPDATE to prevent race condition on single-claim
   446→      const result = await manager
   447→        .createQueryBuilder()
   448→        .update(ShareInvite)
   449→        .set({
   450→          status: 'claimed',
   451→          claimedBy: claimerId,
   452→          claimCount: () => 'claim_count + 1',
   453→        })
   454→        .where('token = :token', { token })
   455→        .andWhere('status = :status', { status: 'active' })
   456→        .andWhere('claim_count < max_claims')
   457→        .andWhere('expires_at > NOW()')
   458→        .execute();
   459→
   460→      if (!result.affected || result.affected < 1) {
   461→        throw new ConflictException('Invite already claimed, expired, or revoked');
   462→      }
   463→
   464→      // Check for existing active share (same sharer, recipient, ipnsName)
   465→      const existingShare = await manager.findOne(Share, {
   466→        where: {
   467→          sharerId: invite.sharerId,
   468→          recipientId: claimerId,
   469→          ipnsName: invite.ipnsName,
   470→          revokedAt: IsNull(),
   471→        },
   472→      });
   473→
   474→      if (existingShare) {
   475→        this.logger.warn(
   476→          `Invite claim for ${invite.ipnsName}: share already exists between ${invite.sharerId} and ${claimerId}`
   477→        );
   478→        return { shareId: existingShare.id };
   479→      }
   480→
   481→      // Clean up any revoked-but-not-yet-rotated records for this triple
   482→      const revoked = await manager.find(Share, {
   483→        where: {
   484→          sharerId: invite.sharerId,
   485→          recipientId: claimerId,
   486→          ipnsName: invite.ipnsName,
   487→          revokedAt: Not(IsNull()),
   488→        },
   489→      });
   490→      if (revoked.length > 0) {
   491→        await manager.remove(revoked);
   492→      }
   493→
   494→      // Create Share record with re-wrapped keys from the claim DTO
   495→      const share = manager.create(Share, {
   496→        sharerId: invite.sharerId,
   497→        recipientId: claimerId,
   498→        itemType: invite.itemType,
   499→        ipnsName: invite.ipnsName,
   500→        itemName: invite.itemName,
   501→        encryptedKey: Buffer.from(dto.encryptedKey, 'hex'),
   502→        hiddenByRecipient: false,
   503→        revokedAt: null,
   504→      });
   505→
   506→      const savedShare = await manager.save(share);
   507→
   508→      // Create ShareKey records from re-wrapped child keys
   509→      if (dto.childKeys && dto.childKeys.length > 0) {
   510→        const shareKeys = dto.childKeys.map((ck) =>
   511→          manager.create(ShareKey, {
   512→            shareId: savedShare.id,
   513→            keyType: ck.keyType,
   514→            itemId: ck.itemId,
   515→            encryptedKey: Buffer.from(ck.encryptedKey, 'hex'),
   516→          })
   517→        );
   518→        await manager.save(shareKeys);
   519→      }
   520→
   521→      return { shareId: savedShare.id };
   522→    });
   523→  }
   524→
   525→  /**
   526→   * Get active invites for a specific item created by a sharer.
   527→   * Auto-cleans expired invites during the query.
   528→   */
   529→  async getInvitesForItem(sharerId: string, ipnsName: string): Promise<ShareInvite[]> {
   530→    const invites = await this.inviteRepo.find({
   531→      where: {
   532→        sharerId,
   533→        ipnsName,
   534→        status: 'active',
   535→      },
   536→      order: { createdAt: 'DESC' },
   537→    });
   538→
   539→    // Auto-clean expired invites
   540→    const now = new Date();
   541→    const expired = invites.filter((inv) => inv.expiresAt < now);
   542→    const active = invites.filter((inv) => inv.expiresAt >= now);
   543→
   544→    if (expired.length > 0) {
   545→      await this.inviteRepo.remove(expired);
   546→    }
   547→
   548→    return active;
   549→  }
   550→
   551→  /**
   552→   * Revoke an invite link. Only the original sharer can revoke.
   553→   * Already-claimed shares are unaffected.
   554→   */
   555→  async revokeInvite(inviteId: string, sharerId: string): Promise<void> {
   556→    const invite = await this.inviteRepo.findOne({ where: { id: inviteId } });
   557→
   558→    if (!invite) {
   559→      throw new NotFoundException('Invite not found');
   560→    }
   561→
   562→    if (invite.sharerId !== sharerId) {
   563→      throw new ForbiddenException('Only the sharer can revoke an invite');
   564→    }
   565→
   566→    invite.status = 'revoked';
   567→    await this.inviteRepo.save(invite);
   568→  }
   569→}
   570→

     1→//! Tauri IPC commands for the desktop auth flow.
     2→//!
     3→//! These commands are invoked from the webview (TypeScript) via Tauri's
     4→//! `invoke()` API. They handle authentication, vault key decryption,
     5→//! Keychain storage, and logout.
     6→
     7→use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicU32, Ordering};
     8→use std::sync::Arc;
     9→use tauri::{Manager, State};
    10→
    11→/// Counter for unique OAuth popup window labels (shared with tray handler).
    12→static POPUP_COUNTER: AtomicU32 = AtomicU32::new(0);
    13→
    14→use zeroize::Zeroizing;
    15→
    16→use crate::api::{auth, types};
    17→use crate::crypto;
    18→use crate::state::AppState;
    19→
    20→/// Handle completed Web3Auth authentication from the webview.
    21→///
    22→/// Called after the webview has completed the Web3Auth SDK flow and obtained
    23→/// an idToken and the user's secp256k1 private key. This command:
    24→/// 1. Sends idToken to backend to get access + refresh tokens
    25→/// 2. Stores refresh token in macOS Keychain
    26→/// 3. Stores private key and derived public key in AppState (memory only)
    27→/// 4. Fetches and decrypts vault keys (including root IPNS keypair)
    28→#[tauri::command]
    29→pub async fn handle_auth_complete(
    30→    app: tauri::AppHandle,
    31→    state: State<'_, AppState>,
    32→    id_token: String,
    33→    private_key: String,
    34→) -> Result<(), String> {
    35→    log::info!("Handling auth completion from webview");
    36→
    37→    // Update tray status: Mounting (auth in progress, about to mount)
    38→    let _ = crate::tray::update_tray_status(&app, &crate::tray::TrayStatus::Mounting);
    39→
    40→    // 1. Convert private key from hex to bytes and derive public key
    41→    //    (needed for the login request)
    42→    let private_key_hex = if private_key.starts_with("0x") {
    43→        &private_key[2..]
    44→    } else {
    45→        &private_key
    46→    };
    47→    let private_key_bytes =
    48→        hex::decode(private_key_hex).map_err(|_| "Invalid private key hex".to_string())?;
    49→    if private_key_bytes.len() != 32 {
    50→        return Err("Private key must be 32 bytes".to_string());
    51→    }
    52→
    53→    // Derive uncompressed public key from private key (65 bytes, 0x04 prefix)
    54→    // Used for both ECIES operations and backend auth (backend expects uncompressed)
    55→    let public_key_bytes = derive_public_key(&private_key_bytes)?;
    56→    let public_key_hex = hex::encode(&public_key_bytes); // 130 hex chars
    57→
    58→    // 2. Login with backend (requires uncompressed publicKey, 130 hex chars)
    59→    let login_req = types::LoginRequest {
    60→        id_token: id_token.clone(),
    61→        public_key: public_key_hex,
    62→        login_type: "corekit".to_string(),
    63→    };
    64→
    65→    let resp = state
    66→        .api
    67→        .post("/auth/login", &login_req)
    68→        .await
    69→        .map_err(|e| format!("Login request failed: {}", e))?;
    70→
    71→    if !resp.status().is_success() {
    72→        let status = resp.status();
    73→        let body = resp.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
    74→        return Err(format!("Login failed ({}): {}", status, body));
    75→    }
    76→
    77→    let login_resp: types::LoginResponse = resp
    78→        .json()
    79→        .await
    80→        .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to parse login response: {}", e))?;
    81→
    82→    // Delegate to shared post-auth setup
    83→    complete_auth_setup(
    84→        &app,
    85→        &state,
    86→        login_resp.access_token,
    87→        login_resp.refresh_token,
    88→        Zeroizing::new(private_key_bytes),
    89→        public_key_bytes,
    90→        login_resp.is_new_user,
    91→        false,
    92→    )
    93→    .await
    94→}
    95→
    96→/// Shared post-auth setup used by both `handle_auth_complete` and `handle_test_login_complete`.
    97→///
    98→/// Stores tokens and keys, initializes/fetches vault, registers device, mounts FUSE,
    99→/// and hides the login window.
   100→async fn complete_auth_setup(
   101→    app: &tauri::AppHandle,
   102→    state: &AppState,
   103→    access_token: String,
   104→    refresh_token: String,
   105→    private_key_bytes: Zeroizing<Vec<u8>>,
   106→    public_key_bytes: Vec<u8>,
   107→    is_new_user: bool,
   108→    skip_keychain: bool,
   109→) -> Result<(), String> {
   110→    // 1. Store access token in API client
   111→    state.api.set_access_token(access_token.clone()).await;
   112→
   113→    // 2. Extract user ID from JWT claims (decode payload, read `sub`)
   114→    let user_id = extract_user_id_from_jwt(&access_token)?;
   115→    *state.user_id.write().await = Some(user_id.clone());
   116→
   117→    // 3. Store refresh token in Keychain (skip in test-login mode to avoid popups)
   118→    if !skip_keychain {
   119→        auth::store_refresh_token(&user_id, &refresh_token)
   120→            .map_err(|e| format!("Keychain store failed: {}", e))?;
   121→        auth::store_user_id(&user_id)
   122→            .map_err(|e| format!("Keychain store user ID failed: {}", e))?;
   123→    } else {
   124→        log::info!("Skipping Keychain storage (test-login mode)");
   125→    }
   126→
   127→    // 4. Store keys in AppState
   128→    *state.private_key.write().await = Some(private_key_bytes.to_vec());
   129→    *state.public_key.write().await = Some(public_key_bytes.clone());
   130→
   131→    // 5. Initialize vault for new users, or fetch existing vault
   132→    //    Also handle the edge case where user exists but vault was deleted.
   133→    if is_new_user {
   134→        log::info!("New user detected, initializing vault");
   135→        initialize_vault(state, &public_key_bytes).await?;
   136→    }
   137→    match fetch_and_decrypt_vault(state).await {
   138→        Ok(()) => {}
   139→        Err(e) if e.contains("404") && !is_new_user => {
   140→            log::warn!("Vault not found for existing user, re-initializing");
   141→            initialize_vault(state, &public_key_bytes).await?;
   142→            fetch_and_decrypt_vault(state).await?;
   143→        }
   144→        Err(e) => return Err(e),
   145→    }
   146→
   147→    // 6. Mark as authenticated
   148→    *state.is_authenticated.write().await = true;
   149→
   150→    // 7. Mount filesystem (or just mark as synced if no filesystem feature enabled)
   151→    // NOTE: Device registry spawn moved AFTER mount to avoid concurrent HTTP
   152→    //       requests that cause reqwest connection pool starvation during pre-populate.
   153→    #[cfg(not(any(feature = "fuse", feature = "winfsp")))]
   154→    {
   155→        let _ = crate::tray::update_tray_status(app, &crate::tray::TrayStatus::Synced);
   156→    }
   157→    #[cfg(any(feature = "fuse", feature = "winfsp"))]
   158→    {
   159→        *state.mount_status.write().await = crate::state::MountStatus::Mounting;
   160→        let private_key = state
   161→            .private_key
   162→            .read()
   163→            .await
   164→            .as_ref()
   165→            .ok_or("Private key not available for filesystem mount")?
   166→            .clone();
   167→        let public_key = state
   168→            .public_key
   169→            .read()
   170→            .await
   171→            .as_ref()
   172→            .ok_or("Public key not available for filesystem mount")?
   173→            .clone();
   174→        let root_folder_key = state
   175→            .root_folder_key
   176→            .read()
   177→            .await
   178→            .as_ref()
   179→            .ok_or("Root folder key not available for filesystem mount")?
   180→            .clone();
   181→        let root_ipns_name = state
   182→            .root_ipns_name
   183→            .read()
   184→            .await
   185→            .as_ref()
   186→            .ok_or("Root IPNS name not available for filesystem mount")?
   187→            .clone();
   188→        let root_ipns_private_key = state.root_ipns_private_key.read().await.clone();
   189→
   190→        // Extract TEE keys for new folder creation
   191→        let tee_keys = state.tee_keys.read().await;
   192→        let tee_public_key = tee_keys.as_ref().and_then(|tk| {
   193→            hex::decode(&tk.current_public_key).ok()
   194→        });
   195→        let tee_key_epoch = tee_keys.as_ref().map(|tk| tk.current_epoch);
   196→        drop(tee_keys);
   197→
   198→        let rt = tokio::runtime::Handle::current();
   199→        match crate::fuse::mount_filesystem(
   200→            state,
   201→            rt,
   202→            private_key,
   203→            public_key,
   204→            root_folder_key,
   205→            root_ipns_name,
   206→            root_ipns_private_key,
   207→            tee_public_key,
   208→            tee_key_epoch,
   209→        ).await {
   210→            Ok(_handle) => {
   211→                *state.mount_status.write().await = crate::state::MountStatus::Mounted;
   212→                let _ = crate::tray::update_tray_status(app, &crate::tray::TrayStatus::Synced);
   213→                log::info!("Filesystem mounted at {}", crate::fuse::mount_point().display());
   214→            }
   215→            Err(e) => {
   216→                let err_msg = format!("Filesystem mount failed: {}", e);
   217→                *state.mount_status.write().await =
   218→                    crate::state::MountStatus::Error(err_msg.clone());
   219→                let _ = crate::tray::update_tray_status(
   220→                    app,
   221→                    &crate::tray::TrayStatus::Error(err_msg.clone()),
   222→                );
   223→                log::error!("{}", err_msg);
   224→                // Don't fail auth -- user is authenticated but mount failed
   225→            }
   226→        }
   227→    }
   228→
   229→    // 8. Register device in encrypted registry (non-blocking, after mount)
   230→    {
   231→        let reg_api = state.api.clone();
   232→        let reg_private_key = Zeroizing::new(private_key_bytes.to_vec());
   233→        let reg_public_key = public_key_bytes.clone();
   234→        let reg_user_id = user_id.clone();
   235→        tokio::spawn(async move {
   236→            let pk_arr: [u8; 32] = match reg_private_key.as_slice().try_into() {
   237→                Ok(arr) => arr,
   238→                Err(_) => {
   239→                    log::warn!("Device registry: invalid private key length");
   240→                    return;
   241→                }
   242→            };
   243→            match crate::registry::register_device(
   244→                &reg_api,
   245→                &pk_arr,
   246→                &reg_public_key,
   247→                &reg_user_id,
   248→            )
   249→            .await
   250→            {
   251→                Ok(()) => log::info!("Device registry updated"),
   252→                Err(e) => log::warn!("Device registry update failed (non-blocking): {}", e),
   253→            }
   254→        });
   255→    }
   256→
   257→    // Close OAuth popup windows and hide the login webview
   258→    for (label, window) in app.webview_windows() {
   259→        if label.starts_with("oauth-popup-") {
   260→            let _ = window.destroy();
   261→        } else if label == "main" {
   262→            let _ = window.hide();
   263→        }
   264→    }
   265→
   266→    log::info!("Authentication complete for user {}", user_id);
   267→    Ok(())
   268→}
   269→
   270→/// Handle session restore when Core Kit has a restored LOGGED_IN session.
   271→///
   272→/// Called when Core Kit's init() restores a previous session from localStorage
   273→/// and the Rust side already has a valid access token from silent refresh.
   274→/// Unlike `handle_auth_complete`, this skips the `/auth/login` POST since
   275→/// tokens are already valid.
   276→#[tauri::command]
   277→pub async fn handle_session_restore(
   278→    app: tauri::AppHandle,
   279→    state: State<'_, AppState>,
   280→    private_key: String,
   281→) -> Result<(), String> {
   282→    log::info!("Handling session restore from Core Kit");
   283→
   284→    let _ = crate::tray::update_tray_status(&app, &crate::tray::TrayStatus::Mounting);
   285→
   286→    // Convert private key from hex to bytes
   287→    let private_key_hex = if private_key.starts_with("0x") {
   288→        &private_key[2..]
   289→    } else {
   290→        &private_key
   291→    };
   292→    let private_key_bytes =
   293→        hex::decode(private_key_hex).map_err(|_| "Invalid private key hex".to_string())?;
   294→    if private_key_bytes.len() != 32 {
   295→        return Err("Private key must be 32 bytes".to_string());
   296→    }
   297→
   298→    let public_key_bytes = derive_public_key(&private_key_bytes)?;
   299→
   300→    // Get the existing access token (set by try_silent_refresh)
   301→    let access_token = state
   302→        .api
   303→        .get_access_token()
   304→        .await
   305→        .ok_or("No access token available - silent refresh may not have succeeded")?;
   306→
   307→    // Extract user_id from the existing access token to look up the refresh token
   308→    let user_id = extract_user_id_from_jwt(&access_token)?;
   309→    let refresh_token = auth::get_refresh_token(&user_id)
   310→        .map_err(|e| format!("Keychain read failed: {}", e))?
   311→        .ok_or("No refresh token in Keychain")?;
   312→
   313→    complete_auth_setup(
   314→        &app,
   315→        &state,
   316→        access_token,
   317→        refresh_token,
   318→        Zeroizing::new(private_key_bytes),
   319→        public_key_bytes,
   320→        false, // not a new user
   321→        true,  // skip Keychain writes (already stored)
   322→    )
   323→    .await
   324→}
   325→
   326→/// Try to silently refresh the session from a Keychain-stored refresh token.
   327→///
   328→/// On cold start, the private key is NOT available (it requires Web3Auth login).
   329→/// This command refreshes the API session tokens only. The webview still needs
   330→/// to complete Web3Auth login to obtain the private key for vault decryption.
   331→///
   332→/// Returns `true` if the API session was refreshed successfully.
   333→/// Returns `false` if no stored session exists or refresh failed.
   334→#[tauri::command]
   335→pub async fn try_silent_refresh(state: State<'_, AppState>) -> Result<bool, String> {
   336→    log::info!("Attempting silent refresh from Keychain");
   337→
   338→    // Check for stored user ID
   339→    let user_id = match auth::get_last_user_id() {
   340→        Ok(Some(id)) => id,
   341→        Ok(None) => {
   342→            log::info!("No stored user ID, silent refresh skipped");
   343→            return Ok(false);
   344→        }
   345→        Err(e) => {
   346→            log::warn!("Failed to read user ID from Keychain: {}", e);
   347→            return Ok(false);
   348→        }
   349→    };
   350→
   351→    // Get refresh token from Keychain
   352→    let refresh_token = match auth::get_refresh_token(&user_id) {
   353→        Ok(Some(token)) => token,
   354→        Ok(None) => {
   355→            log::info!("No stored refresh token for user {}", user_id);
   356→            return Ok(false);
   357→        }
   358→        Err(e) => {
   359→            log::warn!("Failed to read refresh token from Keychain: {}", e);
   360→            return Ok(false);
   361→        }
   362→    };
   363→
   364→    // POST /auth/refresh with the stored refresh token
   365→    let refresh_req = types::RefreshRequest {
   366→        refresh_token: refresh_token.clone(),
   367→    };
   368→
   369→    let resp = match state.api.post("/auth/refresh", &refresh_req).await {
   370→        Ok(r) => r,
   371→        Err(e) => {
   372→            log::warn!("Refresh request failed (network error): {}", e);
   373→            return Ok(false);
   374→        }
   375→    };
   376→
   377→    if resp.status().as_u16() == 401 {
   378→        // Stale token -- delete from Keychain
   379→        log::info!("Refresh token expired, clearing Keychain");
   380→        let _ = auth::delete_refresh_token(&user_id);
   381→        return Ok(false);
   382→    }
   383→
   384→    if !resp.status().is_success() {
   385→        log::warn!("Refresh failed with status {}", resp.status());
   386→        return Ok(false);
   387→    }
   388→
   389→    let refresh_resp: types::RefreshResponse = resp
   390→        .json()
   391→        .await
   392→        .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to parse refresh response: {}", e))?;
   393→
   394→    // Store new tokens
   395→    state.api.set_access_token(refresh_resp.access_token).await;
   396→    auth::store_refresh_token(&user_id, &refresh_resp.refresh_token)
   397→        .map_err(|e| format!("Keychain store failed: {}", e))?;
   398→    *state.user_id.write().await = Some(user_id.clone());
   399→
   400→    log::info!("Silent refresh successful for user {}", user_id);
   401→
   402→    // NOTE: Private key is NOT restored by silent refresh.
   403→    // The webview must complete Web3Auth login to get the private key.
   404→    // is_authenticated remains false until handle_auth_complete is called.
   405→    Ok(true)
   406→}
   407→
   408→/// Start the background sync daemon.
   409→///
   410→/// Called from the webview after successful auth + mount. Creates the sync channel,
   411→/// stores the sender in AppState for the tray menu, and spawns the daemon.
   412→#[tauri::command]
   413→pub async fn start_sync_daemon(
   414→    app: tauri::AppHandle,
   415→    state: State<'_, AppState>,
   416→) -> Result<(), String> {
   417→    log::info!("Starting background sync daemon");
   418→
   419→    let (tx, rx) = tokio::sync::mpsc::channel::<()>(1);
   420→
   421→    // Store the sender in AppState so the tray "Sync Now" button can trigger syncs
   422→    if let Ok(mut guard) = state.sync_trigger.write() {
   423→        *guard = Some(tx);
   424→    }
   425→
   426→    // Extract shared references the daemon needs from AppState.
   427→    // AppState fields are RwLock which we wrap in Arc for shared ownership
   428→    // between the daemon task and the Tauri-managed state.
   429→    //
   430→    // We use the AppHandle to get the managed state which is already Arc-wrapped by Tauri.
   431→    // The daemon reads root_ipns_name and is_authenticated via the app handle's state.
   432→    let api = state.api.clone();
   433→    let app_handle = app.clone();
   434→
   435→    // Get the root IPNS name -- daemon needs to read it periodically
   436→    let root_ipns_name = state.root_ipns_name.read().await.clone();
   437→
   438→    // Clone values for the daemon's owned copies
   439→    let root_ipns_name_lock = Arc::new(tokio::sync::RwLock::new(root_ipns_name));
   440→    let is_authenticated_lock = Arc::new(tokio::sync::RwLock::new(
   441→        *state.is_authenticated.read().await,
   442→    ));
   443→
   444→    // Spawn sync state bridge: periodically sync auth/ipns state from AppState to daemon
   445→    let bridge_app = app.clone();
   446→    let bridge_root = root_ipns_name_lock.clone();
   447→    let bridge_auth = is_authenticated_lock.clone();
   448→    tokio::spawn(async move {
   449→        loop {
   450→            tokio::time::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5)).await;
   451→            let state = bridge_app.state::<AppState>();
   452→            *bridge_root.write().await = state.root_ipns_name.read().await.clone();
   453→            *bridge_auth.write().await = *state.is_authenticated.read().await;
   454→        }
   455→    });
   456→
   457→    tokio::spawn(async move {
   458→        let mut daemon = crate::sync::SyncDaemon::new(
   459→            api,
   460→            root_ipns_name_lock,
   461→            is_authenticated_lock,
   462→            rx,
   463→            app_handle,
   464→        );
   465→        daemon.run().await;
   466→    });
   467→
   468→    log::info!("Sync daemon spawned");
   469→    Ok(())
   470→}
   471→
   472→/// Get the dev-key if one was provided via CLI (debug builds only).
   473→///
   474→/// Returns `Some(hex_string)` if `--dev-key` was passed at startup, `None` otherwise.
   475→/// The webview can use this to skip Web3Auth login and call `handle_auth_complete`
   476→/// directly with a synthetic identity token or via the test-login endpoint.
   477→#[tauri::command]
   478→pub async fn get_dev_key(state: State<'_, AppState>) -> Result<Option<String>, String> {
   479→    log::info!("get_dev_key invoked by webview");
   480→    let key = state.dev_key.read().await.clone();
   481→    log::info!("get_dev_key returning: has_key={}", key.is_some());
   482→    Ok(key)
   483→}
   484→
   485→/// Log a JS error from the webview to the Rust logger.
   486→/// Used in CI to surface webview errors that would otherwise be invisible.
   487→#[cfg(debug_assertions)]
   488→#[tauri::command]
   489→pub fn log_js_error(context: String, message: String) {
   490→    log::error!("[webview-js] {}: {}", context, message);
   491→}
   492→
   493→/// Handle test-login authentication (debug builds only).
   494→///
   495→/// Called from the webview after a successful `POST /auth/test-login` response.
   496→/// Unlike `handle_auth_complete`, this skips the `/auth/login` POST because
   497→/// test-login already returns access + refresh tokens directly.
   498→///
   499→/// The private key comes from the test-login response (server-generated
   500→/// deterministic keypair), NOT from the CLI `--dev-key` argument.
   501→#[cfg(debug_assertions)]
   502→#[tauri::command]
   503→pub async fn handle_test_login_complete(
   504→    app: tauri::AppHandle,
   505→    state: State<'_, AppState>,
   506→    access_token: String,
   507→    refresh_token: String,
   508→    private_key_hex: String,
   509→    is_new_user: bool,
   510→) -> Result<(), String> {
   511→    log::info!("Handling test-login auth completion (debug mode)");
   512→
   513→    // Update tray status: Mounting
   514→    let _ = crate::tray::update_tray_status(&app, &crate::tray::TrayStatus::Mounting);
   515→
   516→    // Convert private key hex to bytes
   517→    let pk_hex = if private_key_hex.starts_with("0x") {
   518→        &private_key_hex[2..]
   519→    } else {
   520→        &private_key_hex
   521→    };
   522→    let private_key_bytes =
   523→        hex::decode(pk_hex).map_err(|_| "Invalid private key hex from test-login".to_string())?;
   524→    if private_key_bytes.len() != 32 {
   525→        return Err("Private key must be 32 bytes".to_string());
   526→    }
   527→
   528→    // Derive public key from the test-login private key
   529→    let public_key_bytes = derive_public_key(&private_key_bytes)?;
   530→
   531→    // Delegate to shared post-auth setup (skips /auth/login POST and Keychain)
   532→    complete_auth_setup(
   533→        &app,
   534→        &state,
   535→        access_token,
   536→        refresh_token,
   537→        Zeroizing::new(private_key_bytes),
   538→        public_key_bytes,
   539→        is_new_user,
   540→        true, // skip Keychain — test-login re-authenticates each time
   541→    )
   542→    .await
   543→}
   544→
   545→/// Open an OAuth popup window directly from Rust.
   546→///
   547→/// Bypasses `window.open()` which is unreliable on Windows WebView2 (the
   548→/// `NewWindowRequested` event / `on_new_window` handler may silently fail).
   549→/// Instead, the webview calls this command via `invoke()` to create a new
   550→/// Tauri webview window pointing directly at the OAuth URL.
   551→#[tauri::command]
   552→pub async fn open_oauth_popup(app: tauri::AppHandle, url: String) -> Result<(), String> {
   553→    let n = POPUP_COUNTER.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
   554→    let label = format!("oauth-popup-{}", n);
   555→
   556→    let parsed_url: tauri::Url = url
   557→        .parse()
   558→        .map_err(|e| format!("Invalid OAuth URL: {}", e))?;
   559→
   560→    // Allowlist: only HTTPS requests to known OAuth providers are permitted.
   561→    const ALLOWED_HOSTS: &[&str] = &["accounts.google.com"];
   562→    if parsed_url.scheme() != "https" {
   563→        return Err("OAuth URL must use HTTPS".to_string());
   564→    }
   565→    let host = parsed_url.host_str().unwrap_or("");
   566→    if !ALLOWED_HOSTS.contains(&host) {
   567→        return Err(format!("OAuth URL host '{}' is not allowed", host));
   568→    }
   569→
   570→    log::info!("Creating OAuth popup window: {} -> {}", label, host);
   571→
   572→    tauri::WebviewWindowBuilder::new(
   573→        &app,
   574→        &label,
   575→        tauri::WebviewUrl::External(parsed_url),
   576→    )
   577→    .title("Sign in with Google")
   578→    .inner_size(500.0, 700.0)
   579→    .center()
   580→    .build()
   581→    .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to create OAuth popup: {}", e))?;
   582→
   583→    Ok(())
   584→}
   585→
   586→/// Logout: invalidate session, clear Keychain, zero all sensitive keys.
   587→#[tauri::command]
   588→pub async fn logout(app: tauri::AppHandle, state: State<'_, AppState>) -> Result<(), String> {
   589→    log::info!("Logging out");
   590→
   591→    // Unmount filesystem before clearing keys
   592→    #[cfg(any(feature = "fuse", feature = "winfsp"))]
   593→    {
   594→        if let Err(e) = crate::fuse::unmount_filesystem() {
   595→            log::warn!("Filesystem unmount failed (will continue logout): {}", e);
   596→        }
   597→        *state.mount_status.write().await = crate::state::MountStatus::Unmounted;
   598→    }
   599→
   600→    // POST /auth/logout (best-effort, don't fail logout if server unreachable)
   601→    let resp = state.api.authenticated_post("/auth/logout", &()).await;
   602→    if let Err(e) = resp {
   603→        log::warn!("Logout request failed (will continue local cleanup): {}", e);
   604→    }
   605→
   606→    // Delete refresh token from Keychain
   607→    if let Some(ref user_id) = *state.user_id.read().await {
   608→        let _ = auth::delete_refresh_token(user_id);
   609→    }
   610→
   611→    // Zero all sensitive keys in memory
   612→    state.clear_keys().await;
   613→
   614→    // Update tray status
   615→    let _ = crate::tray::update_tray_status(&app, &crate::tray::TrayStatus::NotConnected);
   616→
   617→    log::info!("Logout complete");
   618→    Ok(())
   619→}
   620→
   621→/// Initialize a new vault for a first-time user.
   622→///
   623→/// Generates a root folder AES-256 key and derives a deterministic Ed25519 IPNS
   624→/// keypair via HKDF from the user's private key. ECIES-wraps them with the
   625→/// user's secp256k1 public key, and POSTs everything to `/vault/init`.
   626→async fn initialize_vault(state: &AppState, public_key: &[u8]) -> Result<(), String> {
   627→    // Generate root folder AES-256 key (32 random bytes)
   628→    let root_folder_key = crypto::utils::generate_random_bytes(32);
   629→
   630→    // Derive IPNS keypair deterministically via HKDF from user's private key
   631→    let private_key = state
   632→        .private_key
   633→        .read()
   634→        .await
   635→        .as_ref()
   636→        .ok_or("Private key not available for vault IPNS derivation")?
   637→        .clone();
   638→    let private_key_arr: [u8; 32] = private_key
   639→        .as_slice()
   640→        .try_into()
   641→        .map_err(|_| "Invalid private key length")?;
   642→    let (ipns_private_key, _ipns_public_key, root_ipns_name) =
   643→        crypto::hkdf::derive_vault_ipns_keypair(&private_key_arr)
   644→            .map_err(|e| format!("Vault IPNS derivation failed: {:?}", e))?;
   645→
   646→    // ECIES-wrap keys with user's uncompressed secp256k1 public key
   647→    let encrypted_root_folder_key = crypto::ecies::wrap_key(&root_folder_key, public_key)
   648→        .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to wrap root folder key: {}", e))?;
   649→    let encrypted_ipns_private_key = crypto::ecies::wrap_key(&ipns_private_key, public_key)
   650→        .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to wrap IPNS private key: {}", e))?;
   651→
   652→    // 1. Register vault with backend
   653→    let init_req = types::InitVaultRequest {
   654→        owner_public_key: hex::encode(public_key),
   655→        encrypted_root_folder_key: hex::encode(&encrypted_root_folder_key),
   656→        encrypted_root_ipns_private_key: hex::encode(&encrypted_ipns_private_key),
   657→        root_ipns_name: root_ipns_name.clone(),
   658→    };
   659→
   660→    let resp = state
   661→        .api
   662→        .authenticated_post("/vault/init", &init_req)
   663→        .await
   664→        .map_err(|e| format!("Vault init request failed: {}", e))?;
   665→
   666→    if !resp.status().is_success() {
   667→        let status = resp.status();
   668→        let body = resp.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
   669→        return Err(format!("Vault init failed ({}): {}", status, body));
   670→    }
   671→
   672→    // 2. Create and publish initial empty folder metadata
   673→    //    Without this, FUSE init() can't resolve the root IPNS name.
   674→    log::info!("Publishing initial empty root folder metadata");
   675→
   676→    let empty_metadata = crypto::folder::FolderMetadata {
   677→        version: "v2".to_string(),
   678→        children: vec![],
   679→    };
   680→
   681→    // Encrypt metadata with root folder key
   682→    let folder_key_arr: [u8; 32] = root_folder_key
   683→        .try_into()
   684→        .map_err(|_| "Invalid root folder key length".to_string())?;
   685→    let sealed = crypto::folder::encrypt_folder_metadata(&empty_metadata, &folder_key_arr)
   686→        .map_err(|e| format!("Metadata encryption failed: {}", e))?;
   687→
   688→    // Format as JSON { "iv": "<hex>", "data": "<base64>" }
   689→    let iv_hex = hex::encode(&sealed[..12]);
   690→    use base64::Engine;
   691→    let data_base64 = base64::engine::general_purpose::STANDARD.encode(&sealed[12..]);
   692→    let json_metadata = serde_json::json!({
   693→        "iv": iv_hex,
   694→        "data": data_base64,
   695→    });
   696→    let json_bytes = serde_json::to_vec(&json_metadata)
   697→        .map_err(|e| format!("JSON serialization failed: {}", e))?;
   698→
   699→    // Upload encrypted metadata to IPFS
   700→    let initial_cid = crate::api::ipfs::upload_content(&state.api, &json_bytes).await?;
   701→
   702→    // Create and sign IPNS record (sequence 0, 24h lifetime)
   703→    let ipns_key_arr: [u8; 32] = ipns_private_key.as_slice()
   704→        .try_into()
   705→        .map_err(|_| "Invalid IPNS private key length".to_string())?;
   706→    let value = format!("/ipfs/{}", initial_cid);
   707→    let record = crypto::ipns::create_ipns_record(&ipns_key_arr, &value, 0, 86_400_000)
   708→        .map_err(|e| format!("IPNS record creation failed: {}", e))?;
   709→    let marshaled = crypto::ipns::marshal_ipns_record(&record)
   710→        .map_err(|e| format!("IPNS record marshaling failed: {}", e))?;
   711→    let record_base64 = base64::engine::general_purpose::STANDARD.encode(&marshaled);
   712→
   713→    // Publish IPNS record via backend
   714→    let publish_req = crate::api::ipns::IpnsPublishRequest {
   715→        ipns_name: root_ipns_name.clone(),
   716→        record: record_base64,
   717→        metadata_cid: initial_cid,
   718→        encrypted_ipns_private_key: None,
   719→        key_epoch: None,
   720→    };
   721→    crate::api::ipns::publish_ipns(&state.api, &publish_req).await?;
   722→
   723→    log::info!("Vault initialized and root metadata published for new user");
   724→    Ok(())
   725→}
   726→
   727→/// Fetch vault keys from backend and decrypt them using the user's private key.
   728→///
   729→/// Decrypts:
   730→/// - Root folder AES-256 key (32 bytes) from ECIES-wrapped hex
   731→/// - Root IPNS Ed25519 private key (32 bytes) from ECIES-wrapped hex
   732→///
   733→/// The IPNS public key is derivable from the private key if ever needed.
   734→/// Stores all keys in AppState (memory only).
   735→async fn fetch_and_decrypt_vault(state: &AppState) -> Result<(), String> {
   736→    log::info!("Fetching and decrypting vault keys");
   737→
   738→    // GET /vault
   739→    let resp = state
   740→        .api
   741→        .authenticated_get("/vault")
   742→        .await
   743→        .map_err(|e| format!("Vault fetch failed: {}", e))?;
   744→
   745→    if !resp.status().is_success() {
   746→        let status = resp.status();
   747→        let body = resp.text().await.unwrap_or_default();
   748→        return Err(format!("Vault fetch failed ({}): {}", status, body));
   749→    }
   750→
   751→    let vault: types::VaultResponse = resp
   752→        .json()
   753→        .await
   754→        .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to parse vault response: {}", e))?;
   755→
   756→    // Get private key for decryption
   757→    let private_key = state
   758→        .private_key
   759→        .read()
   760→        .await
   761→        .as_ref()
   762→        .ok_or("Private key not available for vault decryption")?
   763→        .clone();
   764→
   765→    // Decrypt root folder key
   766→    let encrypted_root_folder_key = hex::decode(&vault.encrypted_root_folder_key)
   767→        .map_err(|_| "Invalid encryptedRootFolderKey hex")?;
   768→    let root_folder_key = crypto::ecies::unwrap_key(&encrypted_root_folder_key, &private_key)
   769→        .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to decrypt root folder key: {}", e))?;
   770→    *state.root_folder_key.write().await = Some(root_folder_key);
   771→
   772→    // Decrypt root IPNS private key
   773→    let encrypted_root_ipns_private_key = hex::decode(&vault.encrypted_root_ipns_private_key)
   774→        .map_err(|_| "Invalid encryptedRootIpnsPrivateKey hex")?;
   775→    let root_ipns_private_key =
   776→        crypto::ecies::unwrap_key(&encrypted_root_ipns_private_key, &private_key)
   777→            .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to decrypt root IPNS private key: {}", e))?;
   778→
   779→    // Verify stored IPNS key matches HKDF derivation (consistency check)
   780→    let private_key_arr: [u8; 32] = private_key
   781→        .as_slice()
   782→        .try_into()
   783→        .map_err(|_| "Invalid private key length for HKDF check")?;
   784→    if let Ok((expected_ipns_key, _, _)) = crypto::hkdf::derive_vault_ipns_keypair(&private_key_arr) {
   785→        if root_ipns_private_key != *expected_ipns_key {
   786→            log::warn!("Vault IPNS key mismatch: stored key differs from HKDF derivation");
   787→            // Don't block - proceed with stored key for backward compatibility
   788→        }
   789→    }
   790→
   791→    *state.root_ipns_private_key.write().await = Some(root_ipns_private_key);
   792→
   793→    // Store IPNS name and TEE keys
   794→    *state.root_ipns_name.write().await = Some(vault.root_ipns_name);
   795→    *state.tee_keys.write().await = vault.tee_keys;
   796→
   797→    log::info!("Vault keys decrypted and stored in memory");
   798→    Ok(())
   799→}
   800→
   801→/// Extract the user ID (`sub` claim) from a JWT access token.
   802→///
   803→/// Decodes the JWT payload (base64url) without verification -- the server
   804→/// already verified the token, we just need the `sub` field for Keychain lookup.
   805→fn extract_user_id_from_jwt(token: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
   806→    let parts: Vec<&str> = token.split('.').collect();
   807→    if parts.len() != 3 {
   808→        return Err("Invalid JWT format".to_string());
   809→    }
   810→
   811→    // Decode the payload (second part) -- base64url encoding
   812→    let payload = parts[1];
   813→    // base64url: replace - with + and _ with /, then add padding
   814→    let padded = match payload.len() % 4 {
   815→        2 => format!("{}==", payload),
   816→        3 => format!("{}=", payload),
   817→        _ => payload.to_string(),
   818→    };
   819→    let standard = padded.replace('-', "+").replace('_', "/");
   820→
   821→    let decoded = base64::Engine::decode(&base64::engine::general_purpose::STANDARD, &standard)
   822→        .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to decode JWT payload: {}", e))?;
   823→
   824→    let json: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_slice(&decoded)
   825→        .map_err(|e| format!("Failed to parse JWT payload: {}", e))?;
   826→
   827→    json["sub"]
   828→        .as_str()
   829→        .map(|s| s.to_string())
   830→        .ok_or_else(|| "JWT payload missing 'sub' claim".to_string())
   831→}
   832→
   833→/// Derive an uncompressed secp256k1 public key (65 bytes, 0x04 prefix) from a 32-byte private key.
   834→///
   835→/// Used for ECIES encryption/decryption operations.
   836→fn derive_public_key(private_key: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<u8>, String> {
   837→    let sk = ecies::SecretKey::parse_slice(private_key)
   838→        .map_err(|e| format!("Invalid secp256k1 private key: {:?}", e))?;
   839→    let pk = ecies::PublicKey::from_secret_key(&sk);
   840→    Ok(pk.serialize().to_vec()) // 65-byte uncompressed format
   841→}
   842→
   843→#[cfg(test)]
   844→mod tests {
   845→    use super::*;
   846→
   847→    #[test]
   848→    fn test_extract_user_id_from_jwt() {
   849→        // Create a mock JWT with a known sub claim
   850→        // Header: {"alg":"HS256","typ":"JWT"}
   851→        // Payload: {"sub":"user-123-abc","iat":1700000000}
   852→        let header = base64::Engine::encode(
   853→            &base64::engine::general_purpose::URL_SAFE_NO_PAD,
   854→            b"{\"alg\":\"HS256\",\"typ\":\"JWT\"}",
   855→        );
   856→        let payload = base64::Engine::encode(
   857→            &base64::engine::general_purpose::URL_SAFE_NO_PAD,
   858→            b"{\"sub\":\"user-123-abc\",\"iat\":1700000000}",
   859→        );
   860→        let token = format!("{}.{}.fake-signature", header, payload);
   861→
   862→        let user_id = extract_user_id_from_jwt(&token).unwrap();
   863→        assert_eq!(user_id, "user-123-abc");
   864→    }
   865→
   866→    #[test]
   867→    fn test_extract_user_id_invalid_jwt() {
   868→        let result = extract_user_id_from_jwt("not-a-jwt");
   869→        assert!(result.is_err());
   870→    }
   871→
   872→    #[test]
   873→    fn test_extract_user_id_missing_sub() {
   874→        let header = base64::Engine::encode(
   875→            &base64::engine::general_purpose::URL_SAFE_NO_PAD,
   876→            b"{\"alg\":\"HS256\",\"typ\":\"JWT\"}",
   877→        );
   878→        let payload = base64::Engine::encode(
   879→            &base64::engine::general_purpose::URL_SAFE_NO_PAD,
   880→            b"{\"iat\":1700000000}",
   881→        );
   882→        let token = format!("{}.{}.fake-signature", header, payload);
   883→
   884→        let result = extract_user_id_from_jwt(&token);
   885→        assert!(result.is_err());
   886→        assert!(result.unwrap_err().contains("sub"));
   887→    }
   888→
   889→    #[test]
   890→    fn test_derive_public_key() {
   891→        // Use a known private key and verify the public key is 65 bytes with 0x04 prefix
   892→        let private_key = hex::decode(
   893→            "ac0974bec39a17e36ba4a6b4d238ff944bacb478cbed5efcae784d7bf4f2ff80",
   894→        )
   895→        .unwrap();
   896→
   897→        let public_key = derive_public_key(&private_key).unwrap();
   898→        assert_eq!(public_key.len(), 65);
   899→        assert_eq!(public_key[0], 0x04); // Uncompressed prefix
   900→    }
   901→
   902→    #[test]
   903→    fn test_derive_public_key_invalid_size() {
   904→        let result = derive_public_key(&[0u8; 16]); // Too short
   905→        assert!(result.is_err());
   906→    }
   907→}

    34→/**

    38→function getRootFolderState(
    39→  vaultStore: ReturnType<typeof useVaultStore.getState>,
    40→  folders: Record<string, FolderNode>
    41→): FolderNode | null {
    42→  if (!vaultStore.rootFolderKey || !vaultStore.rootIpnsKeypair || !vaultStore.rootIpnsName) {

    44→  }
    45→
    46→  // If we have an explicit root folder in the tree, use it
    47→  const existingRoot = folders['root'];
    48→  if (existingRoot) return existingRoot;
    49→
    50→  // Otherwise construct from vault state
    51→  return {
    52→    id: 'root',
    53→    name: 'My Vault',
    54→    ipnsName: vaultStore.rootIpnsName,
    55→    parentId: null,
    56→    children: [],
    57→    isLoaded: false,
    58→    isLoading: false,
    59→    sequenceNumber: 0n,
