   101→export function useFolder() {
   102→  const [state, setState] = useState<FolderOperationState>({
   103→    isLoading: false,
   104→    error: null,
   105→  });
   106→
   107→  // Get folder store for reactive updates
   108→  // Note: We use getState() in callbacks to avoid stale closures
   109→  const folderStore = useFolderStore();
   110→
   111→  /**
   112→   * Create a new folder.
   113→   *
   114→   * @param name - Folder name
   115→   * @param parentId - Parent folder ID (null for root, or folder UUID)
   116→   * @returns Created folder entry
   117→   * @throws Error if depth limit exceeded or creation fails
   118→   */
   119→  const handleCreate = useCallback(
   120→    async (name: string, parentId: string | null): Promise<FolderEntry> => {
   121→      setState({ isLoading: true, error: null });
   122→      try {
   123→        const folders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
   124→        const vault = useVaultStore.getState();
   125→        const auth = useAuthStore.getState();
   126→
   127→        // Validate depth limit before creating (FOLD-03)
   128→        const parentDepth = folderService.getDepth(parentId, folders);
   129→        if (parentDepth >= MAX_FOLDER_DEPTH) {
   130→          throw new Error(`Cannot create folder: maximum depth of ${MAX_FOLDER_DEPTH} exceeded`);
   131→        }
   132→
   133→        // Get user's ECIES keypair for vault cryptographic operations (public + private keys stored in memory after login)
   134→        // The public key is used here for key wrapping; the private key remains client-side for decryption operations.
   135→        if (!auth.vaultKeypair) {
   136→          throw new Error('No ECIES keypair available - please log in again');
   137→        }
   138→        const userPublicKey = auth.vaultKeypair.publicKey;
   139→
   140→        // Create the folder (generates keys, wraps with user public key, TEE-encrypts IPNS key)
   141→        const { folder, ipnsPrivateKey, folderKey, encryptedIpnsPrivateKey, keyEpoch } =
   142→          await folderService.createFolder({
   143→            parentFolderId: parentId,
   144→            name,
   145→            userPublicKey,
   146→            folders,
   147→          });
   148→
   149→        // Get parent folder state
   150→        const parentFolder =
   151→          parentId && folders[parentId] ? folders[parentId] : getRootFolderState(vault, folders);
   152→
   153→        if (!parentFolder) {
   154→          throw new Error('Parent folder not found or vault not initialized');
   155→        }
   156→
   157→        // Add to parent's children
   158→        const newChildren = [...parentFolder.children, folder];
   159→
   160→        // Update parent folder metadata and publish IPNS
   161→        await folderService.updateFolderMetadata({
   162→          folderId: parentFolder.id,
   163→          children: newChildren,
   164→          folderKey: parentFolder.folderKey,
   165→          ipnsPrivateKey: parentFolder.ipnsPrivateKey,
   166→          ipnsName: parentFolder.ipnsName,
   167→          sequenceNumber: parentFolder.sequenceNumber,
   168→        });
   169→
   170→        // First publish for the new folder's own IPNS record with TEE-encrypted key
   171→        // This sends encryptedIpnsPrivateKey to backend for TEE republish enrollment
   172→        await folderService.updateFolderMetadata({
   173→          folderId: folder.id,
   174→          children: [],
   175→          folderKey,
   176→          ipnsPrivateKey,
   177→          ipnsName: folder.ipnsName,
   178→          sequenceNumber: 0n,
   179→          encryptedIpnsPrivateKey,
   180→          keyEpoch,
   181→        });
   182→
   183→        // Update local state - add new folder to tree
   184→        useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderChildren(parentFolder.id, newChildren);
   185→
   186→        // Also add the new folder node to the store (with its decrypted keys)
   187→        const newFolderNode: FolderNode = {
   188→          id: folder.id,
   189→          name: folder.name,
   190→          ipnsName: folder.ipnsName,
   191→          parentId: parentFolder.id,
   192→          children: [],
   193→          isLoaded: true,
   194→          isLoading: false,
   195→          sequenceNumber: 0n,
   196→          folderKey,
   197→          ipnsPrivateKey,
   198→        };
   199→        useFolderStore.getState().setFolder(newFolderNode);
   200→
   201→        // Post-create: re-wrap new subfolder key for share recipients (fire-and-forget)
   202→        reWrapForRecipients({
   203→          folderIpnsName: parentFolder.ipnsName,
   204→          folders: useFolderStore.getState().folders,
   205→          currentFolderId: parentFolder.id,
   206→          newItems: [{ keyType: 'folder', itemId: folder.id, plaintextKey: folderKey }],
   207→        }).catch((err) => {
   208→          console.warn('[share] Post-create subfolder re-wrapping failed:', err);
   209→        });
   210→
   211→        setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
   212→        return folder;
   213→      } catch (err) {
   214→        const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to create folder';
   215→        setState({ isLoading: false, error });
   216→        throw err;
   217→      }
   218→    },
   219→    []
   220→  );
   221→
   222→  /**
   223→   * Rename a file or folder.
   224→   *
   225→   * @param itemId - ID of item to rename
   226→   * @param itemType - 'file' or 'folder'
   227→   * @param newName - New name
   228→   * @param parentId - Parent folder ID
   229→   */
   230→  const handleRename = useCallback(
   231→    async (
   232→      itemId: string,
   233→      itemType: 'file' | 'folder',
   234→      newName: string,
   235→      parentId: string
   236→    ): Promise<void> => {
   237→      setState({ isLoading: true, error: null });
   238→      try {
   239→        const folders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
   240→        const vault = useVaultStore.getState();
   241→
   242→        // Get parent folder state
   243→        const parentFolder =
   244→          parentId === 'root' ? getRootFolderState(vault, folders) : folders[parentId];
   245→
   246→        if (!parentFolder) throw new Error('Parent folder not found');
   247→
   248→        if (itemType === 'folder') {
   249→          await folderService.renameFolder({
   250→            folderId: itemId,
   251→            newName,
   252→            parentFolderState: parentFolder,
   253→          });
   254→
   255→          // Update local folder state name
   256→          useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderName(itemId, newName);
   257→        } else {
   258→          await folderService.renameFile({
   259→            fileId: itemId,
   260→            newName,
   261→            parentFolderState: parentFolder,
   262→          });
   263→        }
   264→
   265→        // Update parent's children with new name
   266→        const updatedChildren = parentFolder.children.map((child) => {
   267→          if (child.id === itemId) {
   268→            return { ...child, name: newName, modifiedAt: Date.now() };
   269→          }
   270→          return child;
   271→        });
   272→        useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderChildren(parentId, updatedChildren);
   273→
   274→        setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
   275→      } catch (err) {
   276→        const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to rename';
   277→        setState({ isLoading: false, error });
   278→        throw err;
   279→      }
   280→    },
   281→    []
   282→  );
   283→
   284→  /**
   285→   * Move a file or folder to a different parent.
   286→   *
   287→   * @param itemId - ID of item to move
   288→   * @param itemType - 'file' or 'folder'
   289→   * @param sourceParentId - Current parent folder ID
   290→   * @param destParentId - Destination parent folder ID
   291→   */
   292→  const handleMove = useCallback(
   293→    async (
   294→      itemId: string,
   295→      itemType: 'file' | 'folder',
   296→      sourceParentId: string,
   297→      destParentId: string
   298→    ): Promise<void> => {
   299→      setState({ isLoading: true, error: null });
   300→      try {
   301→        const folders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
   302→        const vault = useVaultStore.getState();
   303→
   304→        // Get source and destination folder states
   305→        const sourceFolder =
   306→          sourceParentId === 'root' ? getRootFolderState(vault, folders) : folders[sourceParentId];
   307→
   308→        const destFolder =
   309→          destParentId === 'root' ? getRootFolderState(vault, folders) : folders[destParentId];
   310→
   311→        if (!sourceFolder || !destFolder) {
   312→          throw new Error('Source or destination folder not found');
   313→        }
   314→
   315→        if (itemType === 'folder') {
   316→          await folderService.moveFolder({
   317→            folderId: itemId,
   318→            sourceFolderState: sourceFolder,
   319→            destFolderState: destFolder,
   320→            folders,
   321→          });
   322→
   323→          // Update the moved folder's parentId in local state
   324→          const movedFolder = folders[itemId];
   325→          if (movedFolder) {
   326→            useFolderStore.getState().setFolder({
   327→              ...movedFolder,
   328→              parentId: destParentId,
   329→            });
   330→          }
   331→        } else {
   332→          await folderService.moveFile({
   333→            fileId: itemId,
   334→            sourceFolderState: sourceFolder,
   335→            destFolderState: destFolder,
   336→          });
   337→        }

   907→        try {
   908→          // 5. Update file metadata and publish new IPNS record
   909→          ({ ipnsRecord, prunedCids } = await updateFileMetadata({
   910→            fileIpnsPrivateKey,
   911→            fileMetaIpnsName: filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName,
   912→            folderKey: parentFolder.folderKey,
   913→            currentMetadata,
   914→            updates: {
   915→              cid: fileData.newCid,
   916→              fileKeyEncrypted: fileData.newFileKeyEncrypted,
   917→              fileIv: fileData.newFileIv,
   918→              size: fileData.newSize,
   919→            },
   920→            createVersion,
   921→          }));
   922→        } finally {
   923→          fileIpnsPrivateKey.fill(0);
   924→        }
   925→
   926→        // 6. Publish only the file IPNS record (folder metadata untouched!)
   927→        await folderService.replaceFileInFolder({
   928→          fileId: fileData.fileId,
   929→          fileIpnsRecord: ipnsRecord,
   930→          parentFolderState: parentFolder,
   931→        });
   932→
   933→        // 7. Update local state -- touch modifiedAt and persist migrated IPNS key if applicable
   934→        const updatedChildren = parentFolder.children.map((child) => {
   935→          if (child.type === 'file' && child.id === fileData.fileId) {
   936→            return {
   937→              ...child,
   938→              modifiedAt: Date.now(),
   939→              ...(migratedIpnsPrivateKeyEncrypted
   940→                ? { ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted: migratedIpnsPrivateKeyEncrypted }
   941→                : {}),
   942→            };
   943→          }
   944→          return child;
   945→        });
   946→
   947→        const store = useFolderStore.getState();
   948→        store.updateFolderChildren(parentId, updatedChildren);
   949→
   950→        // 7b. Lazy migration: persist wrapped IPNS key to folder metadata on IPFS
   951→        if (migratedIpnsPrivateKeyEncrypted) {
   952→          folderService
   953→            .updateFolderMetadata({
   954→              folderId: parentFolder.id,
   955→              children: updatedChildren,
   956→              folderKey: parentFolder.folderKey,
   957→              ipnsPrivateKey: parentFolder.ipnsPrivateKey,
   958→              ipnsName: parentFolder.ipnsName,
   959→              sequenceNumber: parentFolder.sequenceNumber,
   960→            })
   961→            .then(({ newSequenceNumber }) => {
   962→              useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderSequence(parentId, newSequenceNumber);
   963→            })
   964→            .catch((err) => {
   965→              console.warn('Lazy IPNS key migration: folder re-publish failed, will retry:', err);
   966→            });
   967→        }
   968→
   969→        // 8. Only unpin CIDs of pruned versions (excess beyond max 10)
   970→        // Old CIDs stay pinned as version history (VER-01)
   971→        for (const prunedCid of prunedCids) {
   972→          unpinFromIpfs(prunedCid).catch(() => {});
   973→        }
   974→
   975→        // Refresh quota
   976→        useQuotaStore.getState().fetchQuota();
   977→
   978→        setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
   979→      } catch (err) {
   980→        const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to update file';
   981→        setState({ isLoading: false, error });
   982→        throw err;
   983→      }
   984→    },
   985→    []
   986→  );
   987→
   988→  /**
   989→   * Restore a previous version of a file.
   990→   *
   991→   * Swaps the current content with a past version. The current content becomes
   992→   * a new version entry (non-destructive). Publishes updated IPNS record and
   993→   * unpins any pruned version CIDs.
   994→   *
   995→   * @param parentId - Parent folder ID ('root' or folder UUID)
   996→   * @param fileId - File ID (UUID)
   997→   * @param versionIndex - Index of version to restore (0 = newest past version)
   998→   */
   999→  const handleRestoreVersion = useCallback(
  1000→    async (parentId: string, fileId: string, versionIndex: number): Promise<void> => {
  1001→      setState({ isLoading: true, error: null });
  1002→      try {
  1003→        const folders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;
  1004→        const vault = useVaultStore.getState();
  1005→        const auth = useAuthStore.getState();
  1006→
  1007→        if (!auth.vaultKeypair) {
  1008→          throw new Error('No ECIES keypair available - please log in again');
  1009→        }
  1010→
  1011→        // Get parent folder state
  1012→        const parentFolder =
  1013→          parentId === 'root' ? getRootFolderState(vault, folders) : folders[parentId];
  1014→
  1015→        if (!parentFolder) {
  1016→          throw new Error('Parent folder not found or vault not initialized');
  1017→        }
  1018→
  1019→        // Find the FilePointer
  1020→        const filePointer = parentFolder.children.find(
  1021→          (c) => c.type === 'file' && c.id === fileId
  1022→        ) as FilePointer | undefined;
  1023→
  1024→        if (!filePointer) {
  1025→          throw new Error('File not found in folder');
  1026→        }
  1027→
  1028→        // Resolve current file metadata from IPNS
  1029→        const { metadata: currentMetadata } = await resolveFileMetadata(
  1030→          filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName,
  1031→          parentFolder.folderKey
  1032→        );
  1033→
  1034→        // Decrypt the file IPNS private key from FilePointer (or HKDF fallback)
  1035→        const { privateKey: fileIpnsPrivateKey, migratedIpnsPrivateKeyEncrypted } =
  1036→          await getFileIpnsPrivateKey(
  1037→            filePointer,
  1038→            auth.vaultKeypair.privateKey,
  1039→            auth.vaultKeypair.publicKey
  1040→          );
  1041→
  1042→        let ipnsRecord: FileIpnsRecordPayload;
  1043→        let prunedCids: string[];
  1044→        try {
  1045→          // Call restoreVersion service function
  1046→          ({ ipnsRecord, prunedCids } = await restoreVersion({
  1047→            fileIpnsPrivateKey,
  1048→            fileMetaIpnsName: filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName,
  1049→            folderKey: parentFolder.folderKey,
  1050→            currentMetadata,
  1051→            versionIndex,
  1052→          }));
  1053→        } finally {
  1054→          fileIpnsPrivateKey.fill(0);
  1055→        }
  1056→
  1057→        // Publish the IPNS record (folder metadata untouched)
  1058→        await folderService.replaceFileInFolder({
  1059→          fileId,
  1060→          fileIpnsRecord: ipnsRecord,
  1061→          parentFolderState: parentFolder,
  1062→        });
  1063→
  1064→        // Update local folder state (modifiedAt and migrated IPNS key on FilePointer)
  1065→        const updatedChildren = parentFolder.children.map((child) => {
  1066→          if (child.type === 'file' && child.id === fileId) {
  1067→            return {
  1068→              ...child,
  1069→              modifiedAt: Date.now(),
  1070→              ...(migratedIpnsPrivateKeyEncrypted
  1071→                ? { ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted: migratedIpnsPrivateKeyEncrypted }
  1072→                : {}),
  1073→            };
  1074→          }
  1075→          return child;
  1076→        });
  1077→        useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderChildren(parentId, updatedChildren);
  1078→
  1079→        // Lazy migration: persist wrapped IPNS key to folder metadata on IPFS
  1080→        if (migratedIpnsPrivateKeyEncrypted) {
  1081→          folderService
  1082→            .updateFolderMetadata({
  1083→              folderId: parentFolder.id,
  1084→              children: updatedChildren,
  1085→              folderKey: parentFolder.folderKey,
  1086→              ipnsPrivateKey: parentFolder.ipnsPrivateKey,
  1087→              ipnsName: parentFolder.ipnsName,
  1088→              sequenceNumber: parentFolder.sequenceNumber,
  1089→            })
  1090→            .then(({ newSequenceNumber }) => {
  1091→              useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderSequence(parentId, newSequenceNumber);
  1092→            })
  1093→            .catch((err) => {
  1094→              console.warn('Lazy IPNS key migration: folder re-publish failed, will retry:', err);
  1095→            });
  1096→        }
  1097→
  1098→        // Unpin pruned version CIDs
  1099→        for (const prunedCid of prunedCids) {
  1100→          unpinFromIpfs(prunedCid).catch(() => {});
  1101→        }
  1102→
  1103→        // Refresh quota
  1104→        useQuotaStore.getState().fetchQuota();
  1105→
  1106→        setState({ isLoading: false, error: null });
  1107→      } catch (err) {
  1108→        const error = err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Failed to restore version';
  1109→        setState({ isLoading: false, error });
  1110→        throw err;
  1111→      }
  1112→    },
  1113→    []
  1114→  );
  1115→
  1116→  /**
  1117→   * Delete a specific past version from a file's version history.
  1118→   *
  1119→   * Removes the version from metadata, publishes updated IPNS record, and
  1120→   * unpins the deleted version's CID.
  1121→   *
  1122→   * @param parentId - Parent folder ID ('root' or folder UUID)
  1123→   * @param fileId - File ID (UUID)
  1124→   * @param versionIndex - Index of version to delete (0 = newest past version)
  1125→   */
  1126→  const handleDeleteVersion = useCallback(
  1127→    async (parentId: string, fileId: string, versionIndex: number): Promise<void> => {
  1128→      setState({ isLoading: true, error: null });
  1129→      try {
  1130→        const folders = useFolderStore.getState().folders;

  1141→
  1142→        if (!parentFolder) {

  1145→
  1146→        // Find the FilePointer

  1149→        ) as FilePointer | undefined;
  1150→
  1151→        if (!filePointer) {
  1152→          throw new Error('File not found in folder');
  1153→        }
  1154→
  1155→        // Resolve current file metadata from IPNS
  1156→        const { metadata: currentMetadata } = await resolveFileMetadata(
  1157→          filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName,
  1158→          parentFolder.folderKey
  1159→        );
  1160→
  1161→        // Decrypt the file IPNS private key from FilePointer (or HKDF fallback)
  1162→        const { privateKey: fileIpnsPrivateKey, migratedIpnsPrivateKeyEncrypted } =
  1163→          await getFileIpnsPrivateKey(
  1164→            filePointer,
  1165→            auth.vaultKeypair.privateKey,
  1166→            auth.vaultKeypair.publicKey
  1167→          );
  1168→
  1169→        let ipnsRecord: FileIpnsRecordPayload;
  1170→        let deletedCid: string;
  1171→        try {
  1172→          // Call deleteVersion service function
  1173→          ({ ipnsRecord, deletedCid } = await deleteVersion({
  1174→            fileIpnsPrivateKey,
  1175→            fileMetaIpnsName: filePointer.fileMetaIpnsName,
  1176→            folderKey: parentFolder.folderKey,
  1177→            currentMetadata,
  1178→            versionIndex,
  1179→          }));
  1180→        } finally {
  1181→          fileIpnsPrivateKey.fill(0);
  1182→        }
  1183→
  1184→        // Publish the IPNS record (folder metadata untouched)
  1185→        await folderService.replaceFileInFolder({
  1186→          fileId,
  1187→          fileIpnsRecord: ipnsRecord,
  1188→          parentFolderState: parentFolder,
  1189→        });
  1190→
  1191→        // Lazy migration: persist wrapped IPNS key to folder metadata on IPFS
  1192→        if (migratedIpnsPrivateKeyEncrypted) {
  1193→          const updatedChildren = parentFolder.children.map((child) => {
  1194→            if (child.type === 'file' && child.id === fileId) {
  1195→              return { ...child, ipnsPrivateKeyEncrypted: migratedIpnsPrivateKeyEncrypted };
  1196→            }
  1197→            return child;
  1198→          });
  1199→          useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderChildren(parentId, updatedChildren);
  1200→
  1201→          folderService
  1202→            .updateFolderMetadata({
  1203→              folderId: parentFolder.id,
  1204→              children: updatedChildren,
  1205→              folderKey: parentFolder.folderKey,
  1206→              ipnsPrivateKey: parentFolder.ipnsPrivateKey,
  1207→              ipnsName: parentFolder.ipnsName,
  1208→              sequenceNumber: parentFolder.sequenceNumber,
  1209→            })
  1210→            .then(({ newSequenceNumber }) => {
  1211→              useFolderStore.getState().updateFolderSequence(parentId, newSequenceNumber);
  1212→            })
  1213→            .catch((err) => {
  1214→              console.warn('Lazy IPNS key migration: folder re-publish failed, will retry:', err);
  1215→            });
  1216→        }
  1217→
  1218→        // Unpin deleted version CID
  1219→        unpinFromIpfs(deletedCid).catch(() => {});
  1220→
  1221→        // Refresh quota

  1234→  /**

  1240→
  1241→  return {
  1242→    // State

  1244→
  1245→    // Operations

     1→// Prevents additional console window on Windows in release
     2→#![cfg_attr(not(debug_assertions), windows_subsystem = "windows")]
     3→
     4→mod api;
     5→mod commands;
     6→mod crypto;
     7→mod fuse;
     8→mod registry;
     9→mod state;
    10→mod sync;
    11→mod tray;
    12→
    13→use tauri::{Manager, WindowEvent};
    14→use state::AppState;
    15→
    16→/// Check if WinFsp filesystem driver is installed on Windows.
    17→///
    18→/// Queries the Windows Registry (HKLM\SOFTWARE\WinFsp) for the install directory
    19→/// and verifies the winfsp-x64.dll exists at the expected path.
    20→/// Returns true if WinFsp is installed and the DLL is present.
    21→#[cfg(target_os = "windows")]
    22→fn check_winfsp_installed() -> bool {
    23→    use winreg::enums::*;
    24→    use winreg::RegKey;
    25→    let hklm = RegKey::predef(HKEY_LOCAL_MACHINE);
    26→    // WinFsp is a 32-bit installer, so on 64-bit Windows the registry key
    27→    // lives under WOW6432Node. Try the native path first, then WOW6432Node.
    28→    let subkeys = [
    29→        "SOFTWARE\\WinFsp",
    30→        "SOFTWARE\\WOW6432Node\\WinFsp",
    31→    ];
    32→    for subkey in &subkeys {
    33→        if let Ok(key) = hklm.open_subkey(subkey) {
    34→            if let Ok(dir) = key.get_value::<String, _>("InstallDir") {
    35→                let dll_path = std::path::Path::new(&dir).join("bin").join("winfsp-x64.dll");
    36→                if dll_path.exists() {
    37→                    return true;
    38→                }
    39→            }
    40→        }
    41→    }
    42→    false
    43→}
    44→
    45→/// CLI arguments for debug builds only.
    46→/// Allows bypassing Web3Auth login with a hex-encoded secp256k1 private key.
    47→#[cfg(debug_assertions)]
    48→mod cli {
    49→    use clap::Parser;
    50→
    51→    #[derive(Parser, Debug)]
    52→    #[command(name = "cipherbox-desktop")]
    53→    pub struct Args {
    54→        /// Hex-encoded secp256k1 private key for headless auth (debug only)
    55→        #[arg(long)]
    56→        pub dev_key: Option<String>,
    57→    }
    58→}
    59→
    60→fn main() {
    61→    // Load .env from the desktop app root (parent of src-tauri)
    62→    // This shares VITE_* vars between the webview and the Rust backend
    63→    let _ = dotenvy::from_filename("../.env");
    64→
    65→    env_logger::init();
    66→    log::info!("CipherBox Desktop starting...");
    67→
    68→    // Parse CLI args (debug builds only: --dev-key <hex>)
    69→    #[cfg(debug_assertions)]
    70→    let dev_key: Option<String> = {
    71→        use clap::Parser;
    72→        let args = cli::Args::parse();
    73→        if args.dev_key.is_some() {
    74→            log::info!("--dev-key provided: headless auth mode enabled");
    75→        }
    76→        args.dev_key
    77→    };
    78→    #[cfg(not(debug_assertions))]
    79→    let dev_key: Option<String> = None;
    80→
    81→    // API base URL: CIPHERBOX_API_URL > VITE_API_URL > localhost default
    82→    let api_base_url = std::env::var("CIPHERBOX_API_URL")
    83→        .or_else(|_| std::env::var("VITE_API_URL"))
    84→        .unwrap_or_else(|_| "http://localhost:3000".to_string());
    85→
    86→    let app_state = AppState::new(&api_base_url, dev_key);
    87→
    88→    tauri::Builder::default()
    89→        .plugin(tauri_plugin_deep_link::init())
    90→        .plugin(tauri_plugin_autostart::init(
    91→            // MacosLauncher param is macOS-specific; on Windows the plugin
    92→            // uses Registry HKCU\...\Run automatically (param is ignored).
    93→            tauri_plugin_autostart::MacosLauncher::LaunchAgent,
    94→            None,
    95→        ))
    96→        .plugin(tauri_plugin_shell::init())
    97→        .plugin(tauri_plugin_notification::init())
    98→        .manage(app_state)
    99→        .setup(|app| {
   100→            // Hide dock icon -- menu bar only (pure background utility)
   101→            #[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
   102→            app.set_activation_policy(tauri::ActivationPolicy::Accessory);
   103→
   104→            // Check WinFsp runtime is available on Windows
   105→            #[cfg(target_os = "windows")]
   106→            {
   107→                if !check_winfsp_installed() {
   108→                    log::error!("WinFsp not found. Virtual filesystem will not be available.");
   109→                    // Show a dialog warning the user. The app can still launch
   110→                    // (tray, settings work) but mount will fail gracefully.
   111→                    use tauri_plugin_notification::NotificationExt;
   112→                    let _ = app.notification()
   113→                        .builder()
   114→                        .title("CipherBox - WinFsp Not Found")
   115→                        .body(
   116→                            "WinFsp filesystem driver is not installed. CipherBox requires \
   117→                             WinFsp to mount your encrypted vault. Please reinstall CipherBox \
   118→                             or install WinFsp manually from https://winfsp.dev"
   119→                        )
   120→                        .show();
   121→                }
   122→            }
   123→
   124→            // Build the system tray menu bar icon
   125→            let handle = app.handle().clone();
   126→            tray::build_tray(&handle)
   127→                .map_err(|e| {
   128→                    log::error!("Failed to build tray: {}", e);
   129→                    let boxed: Box<dyn std::error::Error> = e.into();
   130→                    tauri::Error::Setup(boxed.into())
   131→                })?;
   132→
   133→            // Initial tray status: NotConnected
   134→            let _ = tray::update_tray_status(&handle, &tray::TrayStatus::NotConnected);
   135→
   136→            // In dev-key mode, auto-create the login webview so the JS auth
   137→            // flow runs immediately without user interaction.
   138→            #[cfg(debug_assertions)]
   139→            {
   140→                let state = handle.state::<AppState>();
   141→                if state.dev_key.blocking_read().is_some() {
   142→                    log::info!("Dev-key mode: auto-creating login webview");
   143→                    let _ = tauri::WebviewWindowBuilder::new(
   144→                        app,
   145→                        "main",
   146→                        tauri::WebviewUrl::App("index.html".into()),
   147→                    )
   148→                    .title("CipherBox")
   149→                    .inner_size(480.0, 600.0)
   150→                    .center()
   151→                    .resizable(false)
   152→                    .visible(false)
   153→                    .on_page_load(|webview, payload| {
   154→                        log::info!(
   155→                            "Webview page load: url={}, event={:?}",
   156→                            payload.url(),
   157→                            payload.event()
   158→                        );
   159→                    })
   160→                    .build()
   161→                    .map_err(|e| {
   162→                        log::error!("Failed to create dev-key webview: {}", e);
   163→                    });
   164→                }
   165→            }
   166→
   167→            log::info!("CipherBox Desktop setup complete (tray icon active)");
   168→            Ok(())
   169→        })
   170→        .on_window_event(|window, event| {
   171→            // Hide the login window on close instead of destroying it.
   172→            // The app is a menu-bar utility — only "Quit" from tray should exit.
   173→            if let WindowEvent::CloseRequested { api, .. } = event {
   174→                if window.label() == "main" {
   175→                    api.prevent_close();
   176→                    let _ = window.hide();
   177→                }
   178→            }
   179→        })
   180→        .invoke_handler({
   181→            #[cfg(debug_assertions)]
   182→            {
   183→                tauri::generate_handler![
   184→                    commands::handle_auth_complete,
   185→                    commands::handle_session_restore,
   186→                    commands::try_silent_refresh,
   187→                    commands::logout,
   188→                    commands::start_sync_daemon,
   189→                    commands::open_oauth_popup,
   190→                    commands::get_dev_key,
   191→                    commands::handle_test_login_complete,
   192→                    commands::log_js_error,
   193→                ]
   194→            }
   195→            #[cfg(not(debug_assertions))]
   196→            {
   197→                tauri::generate_handler![
   198→                    commands::handle_auth_complete,
   199→                    commands::handle_session_restore,
   200→                    commands::try_silent_refresh,
   201→                    commands::logout,
   202→                    commands::start_sync_daemon,
   203→                    commands::open_oauth_popup,
   204→                ]
   205→            }
   206→        })
   207→        .run(tauri::generate_context!())
   208→        .expect("error while running CipherBox Desktop");
   209→}
   210→

    24→/**

    26→ * NO class-level auth guard -- individual endpoints opt in.

    28→@ApiTags('invites')
    29→@Controller('invites')
    30→export class InvitesController {
    31→  constructor(private readonly sharesService: SharesService) {}
    32→
    33→  /**
    34→   * PUBLIC: No auth required -- returns only the invite status.
    35→   * Used by the invite landing page before the recipient logs in.
    36→   * Opaque: no file name, no sharer identity.
    37→   */
    38→  @Get(':token')
    39→  @UseGuards(ThrottlerGuard)
    40→  @ApiOperation({
    41→    summary: 'Get invite status (public)',
    42→    description:
    43→      'Check the status of an invite link. Returns only status -- ' +
    44→      'no file name or sharer identity is revealed before authentication.',
    45→  })
    46→  @ApiParam({ name: 'token', description: 'Invite token (URL-safe base64)' })
    47→  @ApiResponse({
    48→    status: 200,
    49→    description: 'Invite status',
    50→    type: InviteStatusResponseDto,
    51→  })
    52→  @ApiResponse({ status: 404, description: 'Invite not found or expired' })
    53→  async getInviteStatus(
    54→    @Param('token', ParseTokenPipe) token: string
    55→  ): Promise<{ status: string }> {
    56→    const result = await this.sharesService.getInviteStatus(token);
    57→    if (!result || result.status !== 'active') {
    58→      throw new NotFoundException();
    59→    }
    60→    return { status: 'active' };
    61→  }
    62→
    63→  /**
    64→   * AUTHENTICATED: Returns full invite data needed for the claim flow.
    65→   * The client uses the encrypted key ciphertext to unwrap with the
    66→   * ephemeral private key (from URL fragment) and re-wrap with their own key.
    67→   */
    68→  @Get(':token/data')
    69→  @UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard, ThrottlerGuard)
    70→  @ApiBearerAuth()
    71→  @ApiOperation({
    72→    summary: 'Get invite data for claim flow (authenticated)',
    73→    description:
    74→      'Fetch full invite data including encrypted key ciphertext. ' +
    75→      'Requires authentication. Used by the client claim flow.',
    76→  })
    77→  @ApiParam({ name: 'token', description: 'Invite token (URL-safe base64)' })
    78→  @ApiResponse({
    79→    status: 200,
    80→    description: 'Full invite data for claim flow',
    81→    type: InviteDataResponseDto,
    82→  })
    83→  @ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: 'Unauthorized' })
    84→  @ApiResponse({ status: 404, description: 'Invite not found or expired' })
    85→  async getInviteData(@Param('token', ParseTokenPipe) token: string): Promise<{
    86→    status: string;
    87→    encryptedKey: string;
    88→    encryptedChildKeys: Array<{
    89→      keyType: 'file' | 'folder';
    90→      itemId: string;
    91→      encryptedKey: string;

    93→    itemType: string;
    94→    ipnsName: string;
    95→    itemName: string;
    96→  }> {
    97→    const invite = await this.sharesService.getInviteForClaim(token);
    98→
    99→    if (!invite) {

   101→    }
   102→
   103→    return {
   104→      status: invite.status,
   105→      encryptedKey: invite.encryptedKey.toString('hex'),
   106→      encryptedChildKeys: invite.encryptedChildKeys,
   107→      itemType: invite.itemType,
   108→      ipnsName: invite.ipnsName,
   109→      itemName: invite.itemName,
   110→    };
   111→  }
   112→
   113→  /**
   114→   * AUTHENTICATED: Claim an invite by providing re-wrapped keys.
   115→   * Creates Share + ShareKey records via the existing sharing infrastructure.

   117→  @Post(':token/claim')

   119→  @ApiBearerAuth()
   120→  @ApiOperation({
   121→    summary: 'Claim an invite link',
   122→    description:
   123→      'Claim an invite by providing the item key re-wrapped for the recipient. ' +

   125→  })
   126→  @ApiParam({ name: 'token', description: 'Invite token (URL-safe base64)' })
   127→  @ApiResponse({
   128→    status: 201,

    77→    const isDesktop = req.headers['x-client-type'] === 'desktop';
    78→
    79→    if (isDesktop) {

    88→

    93→

   100→  @Post('refresh')
   101→  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
   102→  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Refresh access token using HTTP-only refresh token cookie' })
   103→  @ApiResponse({
   104→    status: 200,
   105→    description: 'Tokens refreshed successfully',
   106→    type: TokenResponseDto,
   107→  })
   108→  @ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: 'Invalid or expired refresh token' })
   109→  async refresh(
   110→    @Req() req: ExpressRequest,
   111→    @Body() body: DesktopRefreshDto,
   112→    @Res({ passthrough: true }) res: Response
   113→  ): Promise<TokenResponseDto> {
   114→    const isDesktop = req.headers['x-client-type'] === 'desktop';
   115→
   116→    let refreshToken: string | undefined;
   117→
   118→    if (isDesktop) {
   119→      // Desktop clients: read refresh token from request body
   120→      refreshToken = body?.refreshToken;
   121→    } else {
   122→      // Web clients: read refresh token from cookie
   123→      refreshToken = req.cookies?.['refresh_token'];
   124→    }
   125→
   126→    if (!refreshToken) {
   127→      throw new UnauthorizedException('No refresh token');
   128→    }
   129→
   130→    const result = await this.authService.refreshByToken(refreshToken);
   131→
   132→    if (isDesktop) {
   133→      // Desktop clients: return new refreshToken in response body (no cookie)
   134→      return {
   135→        accessToken: result.accessToken,
   136→        refreshToken: result.refreshToken,
   137→        email: result.email,
   138→      };
   139→    }
   140→
   141→    // Web clients: set new refresh token in HTTP-only cookie (rotation)
   142→    res.cookie('refresh_token', result.refreshToken, REFRESH_TOKEN_COOKIE_OPTIONS);
   143→
   144→    return {
   145→      accessToken: result.accessToken,
   146→      email: result.email,
   147→    };

   167→    if (!isDesktop) {

   171→
   172→    return this.authService.logout(req.user.id);
   173→  }

{
  "file_path": "/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/api/src/shares/share-invite.service.ts",
  "content": "import {\n  Injectable,\n  NotFoundException,\n  ForbiddenException,\n  ConflictException,\n  Logger,\n} from '@nestjs/common';\nimport { InjectRepository } from '@nestjs/typeorm';\nimport { Repository, DataSource, IsNull, Not } from 'typeorm';\nimport { randomBytes } from 'crypto';\nimport { Share } from './entities/share.entity';\nimport { ShareKey } from './entities/share-key.entity';\nimport { ShareInvite } from './entities/share-invite.entity';\nimport { CreateInviteDto } from './dto/create-invite.dto';\nimport { ClaimInviteDto } from './dto/claim-invite.dto';\n\n/** Default invite expiry: 7 days */\nconst INVITE_EXPIRY_MS = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;\n\n@Injectable()\nexport class ShareInviteService {\n  private readonly logger = new Logger(ShareInviteService.name);\n\n  constructor(\n    @InjectRepository(Share)\n    private readonly shareRepo: Repository<Share>,\n    @InjectRepository(ShareKey)\n    private readonly shareKeyRepo: Repository<ShareKey>,\n    @InjectRepository(ShareInvite)\n    private readonly inviteRepo: Repository<ShareInvite>,\n    private readonly dataSource: DataSource\n  ) {}\n\n  /**\n   * Create an invite record with a random token and 7-day expiry.\n   * The encryptedKey is the item key wrapped with an ephemeral public key.\n   */\n  async createInvite(sharerId: string, dto: CreateInviteDto): Promise<ShareInvite> {\n    const token = randomBytes(16).toString('base64url');\n    const expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + INVITE_EXPIRY_MS);\n\n    const invite = this.inviteRepo.create({\n      token,\n      sharerId,\n      itemType: dto.itemType,\n      ipnsName: dto.ipnsName,\n      itemName: dto.itemName,\n      encryptedKey: Buffer.from(dto.encryptedKey, 'hex'),\n      encryptedChildKeys: dto.encryptedChildKeys ?? null,\n      status: 'active',\n      maxClaims: 1,\n      claimCount: 0,\n      expiresAt,\n    });\n\n    return this.inviteRepo.save(invite);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Get invite status by token (public, no auth).\n   * Auto-expires and hard-deletes if past TTL.\n   * Returns null if not found (controller maps to 'expired').\n   */\n  async getInviteStatus(token: string): Promise<{ status: string } | null> {\n    const invite = await this.inviteRepo.findOne({ where: { token } });\n\n    if (!invite) return null;\n\n    // Auto-expire: delete and return null if past expiry\n    if (invite.status === 'active' && invite.expiresAt < new Date()) {\n      await this.inviteRepo.remove(invite);\n      return null;\n    }\n\n    return { status: invite.status };\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Get full invite data for the claim flow (authenticated).\n   * Returns encryptedKey, encryptedChildKeys, itemType, ipnsName, itemName.\n   * Auto-expires and returns null if past TTL.\n   */\n  async getInviteForClaim(token: string): Promise<ShareInvite | null> {\n    const invite = await this.inviteRepo.findOne({ where: { token } });\n\n    if (!invite) return null;\n\n    // Auto-expire: delete and return null if past expiry\n    if (invite.status === 'active' && invite.expiresAt < new Date()) {\n      await this.inviteRepo.remove(invite);\n      return null;\n    }\n\n    // Only return active invites for the claim flow\n    if (invite.status !== 'active') return null;\n\n    return invite;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Claim an invite: atomic single-claim with Share + ShareKey creation.\n   *\n   * Uses UPDATE ... WHERE to prevent race conditions on concurrent claims.\n   * Self-claim prevention: sharer cannot claim their own invite.\n   * Creates standard Phase 14 Share + ShareKey records from re-wrapped keys.\n   */\n  async claimInvite(\n    token: string,\n    claimerId: string,\n    dto: ClaimInviteDto\n  ): Promise<{ shareId: string }> {\n    // First, look up the invite to get sharer info\n    const invite = await this.inviteRepo.findOne({ where: { token } });\n\n    if (!invite) {\n      throw new NotFoundException('Invite not found or expired');\n    }\n\n    // Pre-transaction expiry / status check so expired/revoked invites\n    // return 404 instead of leaking through to the atomic UPDATE (which\n    // would throw 409 and signal that the token exists).\n    if (invite.expiresAt < new Date()) {\n      if (invite.status === 'active') {\n        await this.inviteRepo.remove(invite);\n      }\n      throw new NotFoundException('Invite not found or expired');\n    }\n\n    if (invite.status !== 'active') {\n      throw new NotFoundException('Invite not found or expired');\n    }\n\n    // Self-claim prevention\n    if (invite.sharerId === claimerId) {\n      throw new ConflictException('Cannot claim your own invite');\n    }\n\n    // Run atomic claim + Share creation inside a transaction so that\n    // a failure after marking the invite as claimed is rolled back.\n    return this.dataSource.transaction(async (manager) => {\n      // Atomic UPDATE to prevent race condition on single-claim\n      const result = await manager\n        .createQueryBuilder()\n        .update(ShareInvite)\n        .set({\n          status: 'claimed',\n          claimedBy: claimerId,\n          claimCount: () => 'claim_count + 1',\n        })\n        .where('token = :token', { token })\n        .andWhere('status = :status', { status: 'active' })\n        .andWhere('claim_count < max_claims')\n        .andWhere('expires_at > NOW()')\n        .execute();\n\n      if (!result.affected || result.affected < 1) {\n        throw new ConflictException('Invite already claimed, expired, or revoked');\n      }\n\n      // Check for existing active share (same sharer, recipient, ipnsName)\n      const existingShare = await manager.findOne(Share, {\n        where: {\n          sharerId: invite.sharerId,\n          recipientId: claimerId,\n          ipnsName: invite.ipnsName,\n          revokedAt: IsNull(),\n        },\n      });\n\n      if (existingShare) {\n        this.logger.warn(\n          `Invite claim for ${invite.ipnsName}: share already exists between ${invite.sharerId} and ${claimerId}`\n        );\n        return { shareId: existingShare.id };\n      }\n\n      // Clean up any revoked-but-not-yet-rotated records for this triple\n      const revoked = await manager.find(Share, {\n        where: {\n          sharerId: invite.sharerId,\n          recipientId: claimerId,\n          ipnsName: invite.ipnsName,\n          revokedAt: Not(IsNull()),\n        },\n      });\n      if (revoked.length > 0) {\n        await manager.remove(revoked);\n      }\n\n      // Create Share record with re-wrapped keys from the claim DTO\n      const share = manager.create(Share, {\n        sharerId: invite.sharerId,\n        recipientId: claimerId,\n        itemType: invite.itemType,\n        ipnsName: invite.ipnsName,\n        itemName: invite.itemName,\n        encryptedKey: Buffer.from(dto.encryptedKey, 'hex'),\n        hiddenByRecipient: false,\n        revokedAt: null,\n      });\n\n      const savedShare = await manager.save(share);\n\n      // Create ShareKey records from re-wrapped child keys\n      if (dto.childKeys && dto.childKeys.length > 0) {\n        const shareKeys = dto.childKeys.map((ck) =>\n          manager.create(ShareKey, {\n            shareId: savedShare.id,\n            keyType: ck.keyType,\n            itemId: ck.itemId,\n            encryptedKey: Buffer.from(ck.encryptedKey, 'hex'),\n          })\n        );\n        await manager.save(shareKeys);\n      }\n\n      return { shareId: savedShare.id };\n    });\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Get active invites for a specific item created by a sharer.\n   * Auto-cleans expired invites during the query.\n   */\n  async getInvitesForItem(sharerId: string, ipnsName: string): Promise<ShareInvite[]> {\n    const invites = await this.inviteRepo.find({\n      where: {\n        sharerId,\n        ipnsName,\n        status: 'active',\n      },\n      order: { createdAt: 'DESC' },\n    });\n\n    // Auto-clean expired invites\n    const now = new Date();\n    const expired = invites.filter((inv) => inv.expiresAt < now);\n    const active = invites.filter((inv) => inv.expiresAt >= now);\n\n    if (expired.length > 0) {\n      await this.inviteRepo.remove(expired);\n    }\n\n    return active;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Revoke an invite link. Only the original sharer can revoke.\n   * Already-claimed shares are unaffected.\n   */\n  async revokeInvite(inviteId: string, sharerId: string): Promise<void> {\n    const invite = await this.inviteRepo.findOne({ where: { id: inviteId } });\n\n    if (!invite) {\n      throw new NotFoundException('Invite not found');\n    }\n\n    if (invite.sharerId !== sharerId) {\n      throw new ForbiddenException('Only the sharer can revoke an invite');\n    }\n\n    invite.status = 'revoked';\n    await this.inviteRepo.save(invite);\n  }\n}\n"
}

null,\n      status: 'active',\n      maxClaims: 1,\n      claimCount: 0,\n      expiresAt,\n    });\n\n    return this.inviteRepo.save(invite);\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Get invite status by token (public, no auth).\n   * Auto-expires and hard-deletes if past TTL.\n   * Returns null if not found (controller maps to 'expired').\n   */\n  async getInviteStatus(token: string): Promise<{ status: string } | null> {\n    const invite = await this.inviteRepo.findOne({ where: { token } });\n\n    if (!invite) return null;\n\n    // Auto-expire: delete and return null if past expiry\n    if (invite.status === 'active' && invite.expiresAt < new Date()) {\n      await this.inviteRepo.remove(invite);\n      return null;\n    }\n\n    return { status: invite.status };\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Get full invite data for the claim flow (authenticated).\n   * Returns encryptedKey, encryptedChildKeys, itemType, ipnsName, itemName.\n   * Auto-expires and returns null if past TTL.\n   */\n  async getInviteForClaim(token: string): Promise<ShareInvite | null> {\n    const invite = await this.inviteRepo.findOne({ where: { token } });\n\n    if (!invite) return null;\n\n    // Auto-expire: delete and return null if past expiry\n    if (invite.status === 'active' && invite.expiresAt < new Date()) {\n      await this.inviteRepo.remove(invite);\n      return null;\n    }\n\n    // Only return active invites for the claim flow\n    if (invite.status !== 'active') return null;\n\n    return invite;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Claim an invite: atomic single-claim with Share + ShareKey creation.\n   *\n   * Uses UPDATE ...     35→    encryptedChildKeys: null,
    36→    status: 'active',

  "prompt": "## Task: Split FUSE operations.rs files by category\n\n**Goal:** Split the large FUSE operations files into category-based modules:\n- `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/fuse/operations.rs` (2,363 lines — macOS FUSE-T)\n- `apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/fuse/windows/operations.rs` (2,489 lines — Windows/Dokany)\n\n### Target Structure for EACH platform:\n1. **`read_ops.rs`** — Read operations: `read`, `open`, `release`, `getattr`, `lookup`, `access`\n2. **`write_ops.rs`** — Write operations: `write`, `create`, `setattr`, `rename`, `unlink`, `rmdir`, `mkdir`, `mknod`\n3. **`dir_ops.rs`** — Directory operations: `readdir`, `opendir`, `releasedir`, `statfs`\n4. **`operations.rs`** — Slimmed down: trait impl that delegates to the category modules, shared types/helpers\n\n### Important Rust Pattern:\n- In Rust, you CANNOT split a `impl Trait for Struct` across multiple files\n- The pattern is: impl methods in operations.rs call helper functions defined in the sub-modules\n- Example:\n  ```rust\n  // operations.rs\n  mod read_ops;\n  mod write_ops;\n  mod dir_ops;\n  \n  impl fuser::Filesystem for CipherBoxFS {\n      fn read(&mut self, req, ino, ...) { read_ops::handle_read(self, req, ino, ...) }\n      fn write(&mut self, req, ino, ...) { write_ops::handle_write(self, req, ino, ...) }\n      // etc.\n  }\n  ```\n- Each sub-module gets functions like `pub(crate) fn handle_read(fs: &mut CipherBoxFS, ...)` that contain the actual logic\n\n### Steps:\n1. Read both `operations.rs` files fully\n2. Read the parent module files to understand how operations.rs is imported\n3. For each platform, identify which methods go in which category\n4. Create the sub-modules with extracted logic as free functions\n5. Slim down operations.rs to delegate to the sub-modules\n\n### Verification:\nRun `cd /Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/apps/desktop/src-tauri && cargo check 2>&1 | tail -20` to verify compilation.\n\nNOTE: If cargo check takes too long or Rust toolchain isn't available, at minimum verify the code compiles syntactically by checking that all referenced functions exist, all imports resolve, and types match.\n",
  "run_in_background": true
}

     7→} from '@nestjs/common';

     9→import { Repository, DataSource, IsNull, Not } from 'typeorm';
    10→import { randomBytes } from 'crypto';
    11→import { Share } from './entities/share.entity';
    12→import { ShareKey } from './entities/share-key.entity';
    13→import { ShareInvite } from './entities/share-invite.entity';

    15→import { ClaimInviteDto } from './dto/claim-invite.dto';
    16→
    17→/** Default invite expiry: 7 days */
    18→const INVITE_EXPIRY_MS = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
    19→
    20→@Injectable()
    21→export class ShareInviteService {
    22→  private readonly logger = new Logger(ShareInviteService.name);
    23→
    24→  constructor(
    25→    @InjectRepository(ShareInvite)
    26→    private readonly inviteRepo: Repository<ShareInvite>,
    27→    private readonly dataSource: DataSource
    28→  ) {}
    29→
    30→  /**
    31→   * Create an invite record with a random token and 7-day expiry.
    32→   * The encryptedKey is the item key wrapped with an ephemeral public key.
    33→   */
    34→  async createInvite(sharerId: string, dto: CreateInviteDto): Promise<ShareInvite> {
    35→    const token = randomBytes(16).toString('base64url');
    36→    const expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + INVITE_EXPIRY_MS);
    37→
    38→    const invite = this.inviteRepo.create({
    39→      token,
    40→      sharerId,
    41→      itemType: dto.itemType,
    42→      ipnsName: dto.ipnsName,
    43→      itemName: dto.itemName,
    44→      encryptedKey: Buffer.from(dto.encryptedKey, 'hex'),
    45→      encryptedChildKeys: dto.encryptedChildKeys ?? null,
    46→      status: 'active',
    47→      maxClaims: 1,
    48→      claimCount: 0,
    49→      expiresAt,
    50→    });
    51→
    52→    return this.inviteRepo.save(invite);
    53→  }
    54→
    55→  /**
    56→   * Get invite status by token (public, no auth).
    57→   * Auto-expires and hard-deletes if past TTL.
    58→   * Returns null if not found (controller maps to 'expired').
    59→   */
    60→  async getInviteStatus(token: string): Promise<{ status: string } | null> {
    61→    const invite = await this.inviteRepo.findOne({ where: { token } });
    62→
    63→    if (!invite) return null;
    64→
    65→    // Auto-expire: delete and return null if past expiry
    66→    if (invite.status === 'active' && invite.expiresAt < new Date()) {
    67→      await this.inviteRepo.remove(invite);
    68→      return null;
    69→    }
    70→
    71→    return { status: invite.status };
    72→  }
    73→
    74→  /**
    75→   * Get full invite data for the claim flow (authenticated).
    76→   * Returns encryptedKey, encryptedChildKeys, itemType, ipnsName, itemName.
    77→   * Auto-expires and returns null if past TTL.
    78→   */
    79→  async getInviteForClaim(token: string): Promise<ShareInvite | null> {
    80→    const invite = await this.inviteRepo.findOne({ where: { token } });
    81→
    82→    if (!invite) return null;
    83→
    84→    // Auto-expire: delete and return null if past expiry
    85→    if (invite.status === 'active' && invite.expiresAt < new Date()) {
    86→      await this.inviteRepo.remove(invite);
    87→      return null;
    88→    }
    89→
    90→    // Only return active invites for the claim flow
    91→    if (invite.status !== 'active') return null;
    92→
    93→    return invite;
    94→  }
    95→
    96→  /**
    97→   * Claim an invite: atomic single-claim with Share + ShareKey creation.
    98→   *
    99→   * Uses UPDATE ... WHERE to prevent race conditions on concurrent claims.
   100→   * Self-claim prevention: sharer cannot claim their own invite.
   101→   * Creates standard Phase 14 Share + ShareKey records from re-wrapped keys.
   102→   */
   103→  async claimInvite(
   104→    token: string,
   105→    claimerId: string,
   106→    dto: ClaimInviteDto
   107→  ): Promise<{ shareId: string }> {
   108→    // First, look up the invite to get sharer info
   109→    const invite = await this.inviteRepo.findOne({ where: { token } });
   110→
   111→    if (!invite) {
   112→      throw new NotFoundException('Invite not found or expired');
   113→    }
   114→
   115→    // Pre-transaction expiry / status check so expired/revoked invites
   116→    // return 404 instead of leaking through to the atomic UPDATE (which
   117→    // would throw 409 and signal that the token exists).
   118→    if (invite.expiresAt < new Date()) {
   119→      if (invite.status === 'active') {
   120→        await this.inviteRepo.remove(invite);
   121→      }
   122→      throw new NotFoundException('Invite not found or expired');
   123→    }
   124→
   125→    if (invite.status !== 'active') {
   126→      throw new NotFoundException('Invite not found or expired');
   127→    }
   128→
   129→    // Self-claim prevention
   130→    if (invite.sharerId === claimerId) {
   131→      throw new ConflictException('Cannot claim your own invite');
   132→    }
   133→
   134→    // Run atomic claim + Share creation inside a transaction so that
   135→    // a failure after marking the invite as claimed is rolled back.
   136→    return this.dataSource.transaction(async (manager) => {
   137→      // Atomic UPDATE to prevent race condition on single-claim
   138→      const result = await manager
   139→        .createQueryBuilder()
   140→        .update(ShareInvite)
   141→        .set({
   142→          status: 'claimed',
   143→          claimedBy: claimerId,
   144→          claimCount: () => 'claim_count + 1',
   145→        })
   146→        .where('token = :token', { token })
   147→        .andWhere('status = :status', { status: 'active' })
   148→        .andWhere('claim_count < max_claims')
   149→        .andWhere('expires_at > NOW()')
   150→        .execute();
   151→
   152→      if (!result.affected || result.affected < 1) {
   153→        throw new ConflictException('Invite already claimed, expired, or revoked');
   154→      }
   155→
   156→      // Check for existing active share (same sharer, recipient, ipnsName)
   157→      const existingShare = await manager.findOne(Share, {
   158→        where: {
   159→          sharerId: invite.sharerId,
   160→          recipientId: claimerId,
   161→          ipnsName: invite.ipnsName,
   162→          revokedAt: IsNull(),
   163→        },
   164→      });
   165→
   166→      if (existingShare) {
   167→        this.logger.warn(
   168→          `Invite claim for ${invite.ipnsName}: share already exists between ${invite.sharerId} and ${claimerId}`
   169→        );
   170→        return { shareId: existingShare.id };
   171→      }
   172→
   173→      // Clean up any revoked-but-not-yet-rotated records for this triple
   174→      const revoked = await manager.find(Share, {
   175→        where: {
   176→          sharerId: invite.sharerId,
   177→          recipientId: claimerId,
   178→          ipnsName: invite.ipnsName,
   179→          revokedAt: Not(IsNull()),
   180→        },
   181→      });
   182→      if (revoked.length > 0) {
   183→        await manager.remove(revoked);
   184→      }
   185→
   186→      // Create Share record with re-wrapped keys from the claim DTO
   187→      const share = manager.create(Share, {
   188→        sharerId: invite.sharerId,
   189→        recipientId: claimerId,
   190→        itemType: invite.itemType,
   191→        ipnsName: invite.ipnsName,
   192→        itemName: invite.itemName,
   193→        encryptedKey: Buffer.from(dto.encryptedKey, 'hex'),
   194→        hiddenByRecipient: false,
   195→        revokedAt: null,
   196→      });
   197→
   198→      const savedShare = await manager.save(share);
   199→
   200→      // Create ShareKey records from re-wrapped child keys
   201→      if (dto.childKeys && dto.childKeys.length > 0) {
   202→        const shareKeys = dto.childKeys.map((ck) =>
   203→          manager.create(ShareKey, {
   204→            shareId: savedShare.id,
   205→            keyType: ck.keyType,
   206→            itemId: ck.itemId,
   207→            encryptedKey: Buffer.from(ck.encryptedKey, 'hex'),
   208→          })
   209→        );
   210→        await manager.save(shareKeys);
   211→      }
   212→
   213→      return { shareId: savedShare.id };
   214→    });
   215→  }
   216→
   217→  /**
   218→   * Get active invites for a specific item created by a sharer.
   219→   * Auto-cleans expired invites during the query.
   220→   */
   221→  async getInvitesForItem(sharerId: string, ipnsName: string): Promise<ShareInvite[]> {
   222→    const invites = await this.inviteRepo.find({
   223→      where: {
   224→        sharerId,
   225→        ipnsName,
   226→        status: 'active',
   227→      },
   228→      order: { createdAt: 'DESC' },
   229→    });
   230→
   231→    // Auto-clean expired invites
   232→    const now = new Date();
   233→    const expired = invites.filter((inv) => inv.expiresAt < now);
   234→    const active = invites.filter((inv) => inv.expiresAt >= now);
   235→
   236→    if (expired.length > 0) {
   237→      await this.inviteRepo.remove(expired);
   238→    }
   239→
   240→    return active;
   241→  }
   242→
   243→  /**
   244→   * Revoke an invite link. Only the original sharer can revoke.
   245→   * Already-claimed shares are unaffected.
   246→   */
   247→  async revokeInvite(inviteId: string, sharerId: string): Promise<void> {
   248→    const invite = await this.inviteRepo.findOne({ where: { id: inviteId } });
   249→
   250→    if (!invite) {
   251→      throw new NotFoundException('Invite not found');
   252→    }
   253→
   254→    if (invite.sharerId !== sharerId) {
   255→      throw new ForbiddenException('Only the sharer can revoke an invite');
   256→    }
   257→
   258→    invite.status = 'revoked';
   259→    await this.inviteRepo.save(invite);
   260→  }
   261→}
