     1→import {
     2→  Injectable,
     3→  NotFoundException,
     4→  ForbiddenException,
     5→  ConflictException,
     6→  Logger,
     7→} from '@nestjs/common';
     8→import { InjectRepository } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
     9→import { Repository, DataSource, IsNull, Not } from 'typeorm';
    10→import { randomBytes } from 'crypto';
    11→import { Share } from './entities/share.entity';
    12→import { ShareKey } from './entities/share-key.entity';
    13→import { ShareInvite } from './entities/share-invite.entity';
    14→import { User } from '../auth/entities/user.entity';
    15→import { CreateShareDto } from './dto/create-share.dto';
    16→import { AddShareKeysDto } from './dto/share-key.dto';
    17→import { CreateInviteDto } from './dto/create-invite.dto';
    18→import { ClaimInviteDto } from './dto/claim-invite.dto';
    19→
    20→/** Default invite expiry: 7 days */
    21→const INVITE_EXPIRY_MS = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
    22→
    23→@Injectable()
    24→export class SharesService {
    25→  private readonly logger = new Logger(SharesService.name);
    26→
    27→  constructor(
    28→    @InjectRepository(Share)
    29→    private readonly shareRepo: Repository<Share>,
    30→    @InjectRepository(ShareKey)
    31→    private readonly shareKeyRepo: Repository<ShareKey>,
    32→    @InjectRepository(ShareInvite)
    33→    private readonly inviteRepo: Repository<ShareInvite>,
    34→    @InjectRepository(User)
    35→    private readonly userRepo: Repository<User>,
    36→    private readonly dataSource: DataSource
    37→  ) {}
    38→
    39→  /**
    40→   * Create a new share record with re-wrapped keys.
    41→   * Validates recipient exists and is not the sharer.
    42→   * Prevents duplicate active shares for the same item/recipient pair.
    43→   */
    44→  async createShare(sharerId: string, dto: CreateShareDto): Promise<Share> {
    45→    // Look up recipient by publicKey
    46→    // Strip 0x prefix if present — DB stores bare hex
    47→    const normalizedPubKey = dto.recipientPublicKey.startsWith('0x')
    48→      ? dto.recipientPublicKey.slice(2)
    49→      : dto.recipientPublicKey;
    50→    const recipient = await this.userRepo.findOne({
    51→      where: { publicKey: normalizedPubKey },
    52→    });
    53→
    54→    if (!recipient) {
    55→      throw new NotFoundException('Recipient not found');
    56→    }
    57→
    58→    if (recipient.id === sharerId) {
    59→      throw new ConflictException('Cannot share with yourself');
    60→    }
    61→
    62→    // Check for existing active share (same sharer, recipient, ipnsName)
    63→    const existing = await this.shareRepo.findOne({
    64→      where: {
    65→        sharerId,
    66→        recipientId: recipient.id,
    67→        ipnsName: dto.ipnsName,
    68→        revokedAt: IsNull(),
    69→      },
    70→    });
    71→
    72→    if (existing) {
    73→      throw new ConflictException('Share already exists for this item and recipient');
    74→    }
    75→
    76→    // Clean up any revoked-but-not-yet-rotated records for this triple
    77→    // so the new share can be created without unique constraint conflicts
    78→    const revoked = await this.shareRepo.find({
    79→      where: {
    80→        sharerId,
    81→        recipientId: recipient.id,
    82→        ipnsName: dto.ipnsName,
    83→        revokedAt: Not(IsNull()),
    84→      },
    85→    });
    86→    if (revoked.length > 0) {
    87→      await this.shareRepo.remove(revoked);
    88→    }
    89→
    90→    const share = this.shareRepo.create({
    91→      sharerId,
    92→      recipientId: recipient.id,
    93→      itemType: dto.itemType,
    94→      ipnsName: dto.ipnsName,
    95→      itemName: dto.itemName,
    96→      encryptedKey: Buffer.from(dto.encryptedKey, 'hex'),
    97→      hiddenByRecipient: false,
    98→      revokedAt: null,
    99→    });
   100→
   101→    let savedShare: typeof share;
   102→    try {
   103→      savedShare = await this.shareRepo.save(share);
   104→    } catch (err: unknown) {
   105→      // Handle race condition: concurrent createShare for the same triple
   106→      if (err instanceof Error && err.message?.includes('duplicate key')) {
   107→        throw new ConflictException('Share already exists for this item and recipient');
   108→      }
   109→      throw err;
   110→    }
   111→
   112→    // Create child keys if provided
   113→    if (dto.childKeys && dto.childKeys.length > 0) {
   114→      const shareKeys = dto.childKeys.map((ck) =>
   115→        this.shareKeyRepo.create({
   116→          shareId: savedShare.id,
   117→          keyType: ck.keyType,
   118→          itemId: ck.itemId,
   119→          encryptedKey: Buffer.from(ck.encryptedKey, 'hex'),
   120→        })
   121→      );
   122→      await this.shareKeyRepo.save(shareKeys);
   123→    }
   124→
   125→    return savedShare;
   126→  }
   127→
   128→  /**
   129→   * Get all active, non-hidden shares received by the user.
   130→   * Includes sharer relation for publicKey display.
   131→   */
   132→  async getReceivedShares(recipientId: string): Promise<Share[]> {
   133→    return this.shareRepo.find({
   134→      where: {
   135→        recipientId,
   136→        revokedAt: IsNull(),
   137→        hiddenByRecipient: false,
   138→      },
   139→      relations: ['sharer'],
   140→      order: { createdAt: 'DESC' },
   141→    });
   142→  }
   143→
   144→  /**
   145→   * Get all active shares sent by the user.
   146→   * Includes recipient relation for publicKey display.
   147→   */
   148→  async getSentShares(sharerId: string): Promise<Share[]> {
   149→    return this.shareRepo.find({
   150→      where: {
   151→        sharerId,
   152→        revokedAt: IsNull(),
   153→      },
   154→      relations: ['recipient'],
   155→      order: { createdAt: 'DESC' },
   156→    });
   157→  }
   158→
   159→  /**
   160→   * Get all re-wrapped child keys for a share.
   161→   * Validates the requesting user is either sharer or recipient.
   162→   */
   163→  async getShareKeys(shareId: string, userId: string): Promise<ShareKey[]> {
   164→    const share = await this.shareRepo.findOne({ where: { id: shareId } });
   165→
   166→    if (!share) {
   167→      throw new NotFoundException('Share not found');
   168→    }
   169→
   170→    if (share.sharerId !== userId && share.recipientId !== userId) {
   171→      throw new ForbiddenException('Not authorized to access this share');
   172→    }
   173→
   174→    return this.shareKeyRepo.find({
   175→      where: { shareId },
   176→      order: { createdAt: 'ASC' },
   177→    });
   178→  }
   179→
   180→  /**
   181→   * Add or update re-wrapped keys for an existing share.
   182→   * Only the sharer can add keys.
   183→   */
   184→  async addShareKeys(shareId: string, sharerId: string, dto: AddShareKeysDto): Promise<void> {
   185→    const share = await this.shareRepo.findOne({ where: { id: shareId } });
   186→
   187→    if (!share) {
   188→      throw new NotFoundException('Share not found');
   189→    }
   190→
   191→    if (share.sharerId !== sharerId) {
   192→      throw new ForbiddenException('Only the sharer can add keys');
   193→    }
   194→
   195→    // Upsert: insert or update encrypted_key for each itemId
   196→    for (const entry of dto.keys) {
   197→      const existing = await this.shareKeyRepo.findOne({
   198→        where: {
   199→          shareId,
   200→          keyType: entry.keyType,
   201→          itemId: entry.itemId,
   202→        },
   203→      });
   204→
   205→      if (existing) {
   206→        existing.encryptedKey = Buffer.from(entry.encryptedKey, 'hex');
   207→        await this.shareKeyRepo.save(existing);
   208→      } else {
   209→        const shareKey = this.shareKeyRepo.create({
   210→          shareId,
   211→          keyType: entry.keyType,
   212→          itemId: entry.itemId,
   213→          encryptedKey: Buffer.from(entry.encryptedKey, 'hex'),
   214→        });
   215→        await this.shareKeyRepo.save(shareKey);
   216→      }
   217→    }
   218→  }
   219→
   220→  /**
   221→   * Soft-delete a share by setting revokedAt.
   222→   * Only the sharer can revoke. ShareKey records are kept for lazy rotation.
   223→   */
   224→  async revokeShare(shareId: string, sharerId: string): Promise<void> {
   225→    const share = await this.shareRepo.findOne({ where: { id: shareId } });
   226→
   227→    if (!share) {
   228→      throw new NotFoundException('Share not found');
   229→    }
   230→
   231→    if (share.sharerId !== sharerId) {
   232→      throw new ForbiddenException('Only the sharer can revoke a share');
   233→    }
   234→
   235→    share.revokedAt = new Date();
   236→    await this.shareRepo.save(share);
   237→  }
   238→
   239→  /**
   240→   * Hide a share from the recipient's view.
   241→   * Only the recipient can hide a share.
   242→   */
   243→  async hideShare(shareId: string, recipientId: string): Promise<void> {
   244→    const share = await this.shareRepo.findOne({ where: { id: shareId } });
   245→
   246→    if (!share) {
   247→      throw new NotFoundException('Share not found');
   248→    }
   249→
   250→    if (share.recipientId !== recipientId) {
   251→      throw new ForbiddenException('Only the recipient can hide a share');
   252→    }
   253→
   254→    share.hiddenByRecipient = true;
   255→    await this.shareRepo.save(share);
   256→  }
   257→
   258→  /**
   259→   * Check if a user with the given secp256k1 public key exists.
   260→   * Used to verify recipient is registered before sharing.
   261→   * Does not expose internal user IDs.
   262→   */
   263→  async lookupUserByPublicKey(publicKey: string): Promise<boolean> {
   264→    // Strip 0x prefix if present — DB stores bare hex
   265→    const normalizedKey = publicKey.startsWith('0x') ? publicKey.slice(2) : publicKey;
   266→    const user = await this.userRepo.findOne({
   267→      where: { publicKey: normalizedKey },
   268→      select: ['id'],
   269→    });
   270→
   271→    return !!user;
   272→  }
   273→
   274→  /**
   275→   * Get shares pending key rotation (revoked but not yet hard-deleted).
   276→   */
   277→  async getPendingRotations(sharerId: string): Promise<Share[]> {
   278→    return this.shareRepo.find({
   279→      where: {
   280→        sharerId,
   281→        revokedAt: Not(IsNull()),
   282→      },
   283→      relations: ['recipient'],
   284→      order: { revokedAt: 'ASC' },
   285→    });
   286→  }
   287→
   288→  /**
   289→   * Hard-delete a share and all associated keys after rotation is complete.
   290→   * Only the sharer can complete the rotation.
   291→   */
   292→  async completeRotation(shareId: string, sharerId: string): Promise<void> {
   293→    const share = await this.shareRepo.findOne({ where: { id: shareId } });
   294→
   295→    if (!share) {
   296→      throw new NotFoundException('Share not found');
   297→    }
   298→
   299→    if (share.sharerId !== sharerId) {
   300→      throw new ForbiddenException('Only the sharer can complete rotation');
   301→    }
   302→
   303→    if (!share.revokedAt) {
   304→      throw new ConflictException('Cannot complete rotation for a non-revoked share');
   305→    }
   306→
   307→    // CASCADE will remove all associated ShareKey records
   308→    await this.shareRepo.remove(share);
   309→  }
   310→
   311→  /**
   312→   * Update the encrypted key on an existing share.
   313→   * Used after lazy key rotation to re-wrap the new folder key for remaining recipients.
   314→   */
   315→  async updateShareEncryptedKey(
   316→    shareId: string,
   317→    sharerId: string,
   318→    encryptedKey: string
   319→  ): Promise<void> {
   320→    const share = await this.shareRepo.findOne({ where: { id: shareId } });
   321→
   322→    if (!share) {
   323→      throw new NotFoundException('Share not found');
   324→    }
   325→
   326→    if (share.sharerId !== sharerId) {
   327→      throw new ForbiddenException('Only the sharer can update share keys');
   328→    }
   329→
   330→    share.encryptedKey = Buffer.from(encryptedKey, 'hex');
   331→    await this.shareRepo.save(share);
   332→  }
   333→
   334→  // ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   335→  // Invite link methods (Phase 15)
   336→  // ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   337→
   338→  /**
   339→   * Create an invite record with a random token and 7-day expiry.
   340→   * The encryptedKey is the item key wrapped with an ephemeral public key.
   341→   */
   342→  async createInvite(sharerId: string, dto: CreateInviteDto): Promise<ShareInvite> {
   343→    const token = randomBytes(16).toString('base64url');
   344→    const expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + INVITE_EXPIRY_MS);
   345→
   346→    const invite = this.inviteRepo.create({
   347→      token,
   348→      sharerId,
   349→      itemType: dto.itemType,
   350→      ipnsName: dto.ipnsName,
   351→      itemName: dto.itemName,
   352→      encryptedKey: Buffer.from(dto.encryptedKey, 'hex'),
   353→      encryptedChildKeys: dto.encryptedChildKeys ?? null,
   354→      status: 'active',
   355→      maxClaims: 1,
   356→      claimCount: 0,
   357→      expiresAt,
   358→    });
   359→
   360→    return this.inviteRepo.save(invite);
   361→  }
   362→
   363→  /**
   364→   * Get invite status by token (public, no auth).
   365→   * Auto-expires and hard-deletes if past TTL.
   366→   * Returns null if not found (controller maps to 'expired').
   367→   */
   368→  async getInviteStatus(token: string): Promise<{ status: string } | null> {
   369→    const invite = await this.inviteRepo.findOne({ where: { token } });
   370→
   371→    if (!invite) return null;
   372→
   373→    // Auto-expire: delete and return null if past expiry
   374→    if (invite.status === 'active' && invite.expiresAt < new Date()) {
   375→      await this.inviteRepo.remove(invite);
   376→      return null;
   377→    }
   378→
   379→    return { status: invite.status };
   380→  }
   381→
   382→  /**
   383→   * Get full invite data for the claim flow (authenticated).
   384→   * Returns encryptedKey, encryptedChildKeys, itemType, ipnsName, itemName.
   385→   * Auto-expires and returns null if past TTL.
   386→   */
   387→  async getInviteForClaim(token: string): Promise<ShareInvite | null> {
   388→    const invite = await this.inviteRepo.findOne({ where: { token } });
   389→
   390→    if (!invite) return null;
   391→
   392→    // Auto-expire: delete and return null if past expiry
   393→    if (invite.status === 'active' && invite.expiresAt < new Date()) {
   394→      await this.inviteRepo.remove(invite);
   395→      return null;
   396→    }
   397→
   398→    // Only return active invites for the claim flow
   399→    if (invite.status !== 'active') return null;
   400→
   401→    return invite;
   402→  }
   403→
   404→  /**
   405→   * Claim an invite: atomic single-claim with Share + ShareKey creation.
   406→   *
   407→   * Uses UPDATE ... WHERE to prevent race conditions on concurrent claims.
   408→   * Self-claim prevention: sharer cannot claim their own invite.
   409→   * Creates standard Phase 14 Share + ShareKey records from re-wrapped keys.
   410→   */
   411→  async claimInvite(
   412→    token: string,
   413→    claimerId: string,
   414→    dto: ClaimInviteDto
   415→  ): Promise<{ shareId: string }> {
   416→    // First, look up the invite to get sharer info
   417→    const invite = await this.inviteRepo.findOne({ where: { token } });
   418→
   419→    if (!invite) {
   420→      throw new NotFoundException('Invite not found or expired');
   421→    }
   422→
   423→    // Pre-transaction expiry / status check so expired/revoked invites
   424→    // return 404 instead of leaking through to the atomic UPDATE (which
   425→    // would throw 409 and signal that the token exists).
   426→    if (invite.expiresAt < new Date()) {
   427→      if (invite.status === 'active') {
   428→        await this.inviteRepo.remove(invite);
   429→      }
   430→      throw new NotFoundException('Invite not found or expired');
   431→    }
   432→
   433→    if (invite.status !== 'active') {
   434→      throw new NotFoundException('Invite not found or expired');
   435→    }
   436→
   437→    // Self-claim prevention
   438→    if (invite.sharerId === claimerId) {
   439→      throw new ConflictException('Cannot claim your own invite');
   440→    }
   441→
   442→    // Run atomic claim + Share creation inside a transaction so that
   443→    // a failure after marking the invite as claimed is rolled back.
   444→    return this.dataSource.transaction(async (manager) => {
   445→      // Atomic UPDATE to prevent race condition on single-claim
   446→      const result = await manager
   447→        .createQueryBuilder()
   448→        .update(ShareInvite)
   449→        .set({
   450→          status: 'claimed',
   451→          claimedBy: claimerId,
   452→          claimCount: () => 'claim_count + 1',
   453→        })
   454→        .where('token = :token', { token })
   455→        .andWhere('status = :status', { status: 'active' })
   456→        .andWhere('claim_count < max_claims')
   457→        .andWhere('expires_at > NOW()')
   458→        .execute();
   459→
   460→      if (!result.affected || result.affected < 1) {
   461→        throw new ConflictException('Invite already claimed, expired, or revoked');
   462→      }
   463→
   464→      // Check for existing active share (same sharer, recipient, ipnsName)
   465→      const existingShare = await manager.findOne(Share, {
   466→        where: {
   467→          sharerId: invite.sharerId,
   468→          recipientId: claimerId,
   469→          ipnsName: invite.ipnsName,
   470→          revokedAt: IsNull(),
   471→        },
   472→      });
   473→
   474→      if (existingShare) {
   475→        this.logger.warn(
   476→          `Invite claim for ${invite.ipnsName}: share already exists between ${invite.sharerId} and ${claimerId}`
   477→        );
   478→        return { shareId: existingShare.id };
   479→      }
   480→
   481→      // Clean up any revoked-but-not-yet-rotated records for this triple
   482→      const revoked = await manager.find(Share, {
   483→        where: {
   484→          sharerId: invite.sharerId,
   485→          recipientId: claimerId,
   486→          ipnsName: invite.ipnsName,
   487→          revokedAt: Not(IsNull()),
   488→        },
   489→      });
   490→      if (revoked.length > 0) {
   491→        await manager.remove(revoked);
   492→      }
   493→
   494→      // Create Share record with re-wrapped keys from the claim DTO
   495→      const share = manager.create(Share, {
   496→        sharerId: invite.sharerId,
   497→        recipientId: claimerId,
   498→        itemType: invite.itemType,
   499→        ipnsName: invite.ipnsName,
   500→        itemName: invite.itemName,
   501→        encryptedKey: Buffer.from(dto.encryptedKey, 'hex'),
   502→        hiddenByRecipient: false,
   503→        revokedAt: null,
   504→      });
   505→
   506→      const savedShare = await manager.save(share);
   507→
   508→      // Create ShareKey records from re-wrapped child keys
   509→      if (dto.childKeys && dto.childKeys.length > 0) {
   510→        const shareKeys = dto.childKeys.map((ck) =>
   511→          manager.create(ShareKey, {
   512→            shareId: savedShare.id,
   513→            keyType: ck.keyType,
   514→            itemId: ck.itemId,
   515→            encryptedKey: Buffer.from(ck.encryptedKey, 'hex'),
   516→          })
   517→        );
   518→        await manager.save(shareKeys);
   519→      }
   520→
   521→      return { shareId: savedShare.id };
   522→    });
   523→  }
   524→
   525→  /**
   526→   * Get active invites for a specific item created by a sharer.
   527→   * Auto-cleans expired invites during the query.
   528→   */
   529→  async getInvitesForItem(sharerId: string, ipnsName: string): Promise<ShareInvite[]> {
   530→    const invites = await this.inviteRepo.find({
   531→      where: {
   532→        sharerId,
   533→        ipnsName,
   534→        status: 'active',
   535→      },
   536→      order: { createdAt: 'DESC' },
   537→    });
   538→
   539→    // Auto-clean expired invites
   540→    const now = new Date();
   541→    const expired = invites.filter((inv) => inv.expiresAt < now);
   542→    const active = invites.filter((inv) => inv.expiresAt >= now);
   543→
   544→    if (expired.length > 0) {
   545→      await this.inviteRepo.remove(expired);
   546→    }
   547→
   548→    return active;
   549→  }
   550→
   551→  /**
   552→   * Revoke an invite link. Only the original sharer can revoke.
   553→   * Already-claimed shares are unaffected.
   554→   */
   555→  async revokeInvite(inviteId: string, sharerId: string): Promise<void> {
   556→    const invite = await this.inviteRepo.findOne({ where: { id: inviteId } });
   557→
   558→    if (!invite) {
   559→      throw new NotFoundException('Invite not found');
   560→    }
   561→
   562→    if (invite.sharerId !== sharerId) {
   563→      throw new ForbiddenException('Only the sharer can revoke an invite');
   564→    }
   565→
   566→    invite.status = 'revoked';
   567→    await this.inviteRepo.save(invite);
   568→  }
   569→}
   570→

    24→/**

    28→@ApiTags('invites')

    32→
    33→  /**

    38→  @Get(':token')

    53→  async getInviteStatus(

    75→      'Requires authentication. Used by the client claim flow.',
    76→  })
    77→  @ApiParam({ name: 'token', description: 'Invite token (URL-safe base64)' })
    78→  @ApiResponse({
    79→    status: 200,
    80→    description: 'Full invite data for claim flow',
    81→    type: InviteDataResponseDto,
    82→  })
    83→  @ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: 'Unauthorized' })
    84→  @ApiResponse({ status: 404, description: 'Invite not found or expired' })
    85→  async getInviteData(@Param('token', ParseTokenPipe) token: string): Promise<{
    86→    status: string;
    87→    encryptedKey: string;
    88→    encryptedChildKeys: Array<{
    89→      keyType: 'file' | 'folder';
    90→      itemId: string;
    91→      encryptedKey: string;
    92→    }> | null;
    93→    itemType: string;
    94→    ipnsName: string;
    95→    itemName: string;
    96→  }> {
    97→    const invite = await this.sharesService.getInviteForClaim(token);
    98→
    99→    if (!invite) {
   100→      throw new NotFoundException('Invite not found or expired');
   101→    }
   102→
   103→    return {
   104→      status: invite.status,
   105→      encryptedKey: invite.encryptedKey.toString('hex'),
   106→      encryptedChildKeys: invite.encryptedChildKeys,
   107→      itemType: invite.itemType,
   108→      ipnsName: invite.ipnsName,
   109→      itemName: invite.itemName,
   110→    };
   111→  }
   112→
   113→  /**
   114→   * AUTHENTICATED: Claim an invite by providing re-wrapped keys.
   115→   * Creates Share + ShareKey records via the existing sharing infrastructure.
   116→   */
   117→  @Post(':token/claim')
   118→  @UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard, ThrottlerGuard)
   119→  @ApiBearerAuth()
   120→  @ApiOperation({
   121→    summary: 'Claim an invite link',
   122→    description:
   123→      'Claim an invite by providing the item key re-wrapped for the recipient. ' +
   124→      'Creates Share and ShareKey records. Single-claim enforced atomically.',
   125→  })
   126→  @ApiParam({ name: 'token', description: 'Invite token (URL-safe base64)' })
   127→  @ApiResponse({
   128→    status: 201,
   129→    description: 'Invite claimed successfully',
   130→    type: ClaimInviteResponseDto,
   131→  })
   132→  @ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: 'Unauthorized' })
   133→  @ApiResponse({ status: 404, description: 'Invite not found or expired' })
   134→  @ApiResponse({ status: 409, description: 'Invite already claimed or self-claim' })
   135→  async claimInvite(
   136→    @Request() req: RequestWithUser,
   137→    @Param('token', ParseTokenPipe) token: string,
   138→    @Body() dto: ClaimInviteDto
   139→  ): Promise<{ shareId: string }> {
   140→    return this.sharesService.claimInvite(token, req.user.id, dto);
   141→  }
   142→}
   143→

    23→/**

    27→@ApiTags('share-invites')

    30→@Controller('shares/invites')
    31→export class ShareInvitesController {
    32→  constructor(private readonly sharesService: SharesService) {}
    33→
    34→  /**
    35→   * Create a new invite link for sharing a file or folder.
    36→   * Returns the invite token for URL construction on the client.
    37→   */
    38→  @Post()
    39→  @ApiOperation({
    40→    summary: 'Create an invite link',
    41→    description:
    42→      'Create a new invite link with the item key wrapped by an ephemeral public key. ' +
    43→      'Returns the invite token for URL construction. Default expiry: 7 days.',
    44→  })
    45→  @ApiResponse({
    46→    status: 201,
    47→    description: 'Invite created',
    48→    type: InviteResponseDto,
    49→  })
    50→  @ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: 'Unauthorized' })
    51→  async createInvite(
    52→    @Request() req: RequestWithUser,
    53→    @Body() dto: CreateInviteDto
    54→  ): Promise<{
    55→    id: string;
    56→    token: string;
    57→    itemType: string;
    58→    ipnsName: string;
    59→    itemName: string;
    60→    status: string;
    61→    expiresAt: Date;
    62→    createdAt: Date;
    63→  }> {
    64→    const invite = await this.sharesService.createInvite(req.user.id, dto);
    65→    return {
    66→      id: invite.id,
    67→      token: invite.token,
    68→      itemType: invite.itemType,
    69→      ipnsName: invite.ipnsName,
    70→      itemName: invite.itemName,
    71→      status: invite.status,
    72→      expiresAt: invite.expiresAt,
    73→      createdAt: invite.createdAt,
    74→    };
    75→  }
    76→
    77→  /**
    78→   * List active (unclaimed, unexpired) invites for a specific item.
    79→   * Requires ipnsName query parameter.
    80→   */
    81→  @Get()
    82→  @ApiOperation({
    83→    summary: 'List active invites for an item',
    84→    description:
    85→      'Get all active (unclaimed, unexpired) invite links created by the authenticated user ' +
    86→      'for the specified item. Expired invites are auto-cleaned.',
    87→  })
    88→  @ApiQuery({
    89→    name: 'ipnsName',
    90→    description: 'IPNS name of the item to list invites for',
    91→    required: true,
    92→  })
    93→  @ApiResponse({
    94→    status: 200,
    95→    description: 'List of active invites',
    96→    type: [InviteResponseDto],
    97→  })
    98→  @ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: 'Unauthorized' })
    99→  async listInvites(
   100→    @Request() req: RequestWithUser,
   101→    @Query('ipnsName') ipnsName: string
   102→  ): Promise<

   109→      status: string;

   111→      createdAt: Date;

   113→  > {

   118→      itemType: inv.itemType,
   119→      ipnsName: inv.ipnsName,
   120→      itemName: inv.itemName,
   121→      status: inv.status,
   122→      expiresAt: inv.expiresAt,
   123→      createdAt: inv.createdAt,
   124→    }));
   125→  }
   126→
   127→  /**
   128→   * Revoke an active invite link.
   129→   * Only the sharer can revoke. Already-claimed shares are unaffected.
   130→   */
   131→  @Delete(':inviteId')
   132→  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.NO_CONTENT)
   133→  @ApiOperation({

   135→    description:

   137→      'Already-claimed shares persist independently.',
   138→  })
   139→  @ApiResponse({ status: 204, description: 'Invite revoked' })
   140→  @ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: 'Unauthorized' })
   141→  @ApiResponse({ status: 403, description: 'Only the sharer can revoke' })
   142→  @ApiResponse({ status: 404, description: 'Invite not found' })
   143→  async revokeInvite(
   144→    @Request() req: RequestWithUser,
   145→    @Param('inviteId', ParseUUIDPipe) inviteId: string
   146→  ): Promise<void> {
   147→    await this.sharesService.revokeInvite(inviteId, req.user.id);
   148→  }
   149→}

```
#!/bin/bash

# Check if API source files are staged but generated client files are not
# This prevents forgetting to run `pnpm api:generate` after API changes

# Files that indicate API changes requiring client regeneration
API_PATTERNS=(
  "apps/api/src/.*\.dto\.ts"
  "apps/api/src/.*\.controller\.ts"
  "apps/api/src/.*\.entity\.ts"
)

# Generated files that should be updated when API changes
GENERATED_FILES=(
  "packages/api-client/openapi.json"
)

# Get staged files
STAGED_FILES=$(git diff --cached --name-only)

# Check if any API source files are staged
API_CHANGED=false
for pattern in "${API_PATTERNS[@]}"; do
  if echo "$STAGED_FILES" | grep -qE "$pattern"; then
    API_CHANGED=true
    break
  fi
done

if [ "$API_CHANGED" = false ]; then
  exit 0
fi

# API files changed - check if generated files are also staged
MISSING_GENERATED=()
for file in "${GENERATED_FILES[@]}"; do
  if ! echo "$STAGED_FILES" | grep -q "^${file}$"; then
    MISSING_GENERATED+=("$file")
  fi
done

if [ ${#MISSING_GENERATED[@]} -gt 0 ]; then
  echo ""
  echo "⚠️  API source files changed but generated client files are not staged!"
  echo ""
  echo "You modified API DTOs, controllers, or entities but didn't regenerate the client."
  echo ""
  echo "Run this command and stage the changes:"
  echo ""
  echo "    pnpm api:generate"
  echo ""
  echo "Missing generated files:"
  for file in "${MISSING_GENERATED[@]}"; do
    echo "    - $file"
  done
  echo ""
  echo "To skip this check (not recommended), use: git commit --no-verify"
  echo ""
  exit 1
fi

exit 0
```

   111→## Architecture Notes (Not Bugs, Monitor)
   112→
   113→- **Desktop `auth.ts` (771 lines) parallels web `useAuth.ts` (510 lines)** — structural duplication from Tauri/browser split. Can't easily share.

{
  "command": "REPLY_BODY='This is a pre-existing pattern that existed in the original monolithic `operations.rs` (line 995). This refactoring PR only moved code between files without changing behavior.\n\nThe write-open path needs existing file content to support appending/modifying. The prefetch+cache-check happens first (line 238-240); the synchronous fallback only triggers on cache miss. This is a deliberate design tradeoff documented in the desktop CLAUDE.md — the alternative (returning immediately and letting `read()` fail) would break write-open semantics since the `OpenFileHandle::new_write()` needs the content at creation time.\n\nCould be revisited in a separate PR if we want to restructure write-open to be lazy, but out of scope for a structural split.'\n\ngh api repos/FSM1/cipher-box/pulls/249/comments \\\n  --field in_reply_to=2874310880 \\\n  --raw-field body=\"$REPLY_BODY\"",
  "description": "Reply to thread 1: open sync fetch"
}

This is a deliberate design tradeoff documented in the desktop CLAUDE.md — the alternative (returning immediately and letting `read()` fail) would break write-open semantics since the `OpenFileHandle::new_write()` needs the content at creation time.\n\nCould be revisited in a separate PR if we want to restructure write-open to be lazy, but out of scope for a structural split.","created_at":"2026-03-02T19:48:35Z","updated_at":"2026-03-02T19:48:35Z","html_url":"https://github.com/FSM1/cipher-box/pull/249#discussion_r2874343676","pull_request_url":"https://api.github.com/repos/FSM1/cipher-box/pulls/249","_links":{"self":{"href":"https://api.github.com/repos/FSM1/cipher-box/pulls/comments/2874343676"},"html":{"href":"https://github.com/FSM1/cipher-box/pull/249#discussion_r2874343676"},"pull_request":{"href":"https://api.github.com/repos/FSM1/cipher-box/pulls/249"}},"reactions":{"url":"https://api.github.com/repos/FSM1/cipher-box/pulls/comments/2874343676/reactions","total_count":0,"+1":0,"-1":0,"laugh":0,"hooray":0,"confused":0,"heart":0,"rocket":0,"eyes":0},"start_line":237,"original_start_line":237,"start_side":"RIGHT","line":249,"original_line":249,"side":"RIGHT","in_reply_to_id":2874310880,"author_association":"OWNER","original_position":249,"position":249,"subject_type":"line"}

  "command": "# Thread 2: read_ops.rs:501 - read polling loop\ngh api repos/FSM1/cipher-box/pulls/249/comments \\\n  --field in_reply_to=2874310884 \\\n  --raw-field body='Pre-existing pattern (original `operations.rs` lines 1322-1342). This PR only moved code without changing behavior.\n\nThe 3-second poll loop is a deliberate design choice for the FUSE-T SMB backend. On cache miss, `read()` has already fired an async prefetch (lines 420-473) — the loop waits for it to arrive via the `mpsc` channel. Returning EIO immediately would cause macOS SMB to surface an error dialog to the user for every first read of an uncached file. The brief poll gives the prefetch time to complete before falling back to EIO (which SMB will retry).\n\nValid improvement to explore in a follow-up, but out of scope for this structural split.'",
  "description": "Reply to thread 2: read polling loop"
}

This PR only moved code without changing behavior.\n\nThe inode insert + parent update happen on lines 113-120, before fallible operations on lines 122-136. However, the network I/O is spawned in a background thread (line 143) and the function returns `Ok` on line 203 regardless of the background result. The error path on lines 212-215 only triggers if `encrypt_metadata_to_json` or `build_folder_metadata` fails — which are pure local crypto/state operations that rarely fail.\n\nThis is a valid robustness improvement but should be addressed in a separate PR since it requires careful rollback logic (ino removal, parent children cleanup, timestamp restore). Filed as a pre-existing issue, not introduced by this split.'",

{
  "command": "# Thread 5: write_ops.rs:117 - guard negative offsets\ngh api repos/FSM1/cipher-box/pulls/249/comments \\\n  --field in_reply_to=2874310896 \\\n  --raw-field body='Pre-existing pattern (original `operations.rs` line 1121). This PR only moved code without changing behavior.\n\nOn the macOS FUSE path, `offset` comes from `fuser::Filesystem::write()` which passes `i64`. In practice, FUSE never sends negative offsets — the kernel validates this before the callback. Using `data.len()` vs `written` is also safe here because `write_at()` writes the entire buffer to the temp file (it uses `File::write_all` internally, which loops until complete or errors).\n\nThat said, using `saturating_add` and `written` instead of `data.len()` would be more defensive. Could be addressed in a follow-up hardening PR, but out of scope for this structural split.'",
  "description": "Reply to thread 5: offset guard"
}

{
  "command": "# Thread 6: write_ops.rs:303 - direct IPFS work in mutation callbacks\ngh api repos/FSM1/cipher-box/pulls/249/comments \\\n  --field in_reply_to=2874310899 \\\n  --raw-field body='Pre-existing pattern (original `operations.rs` line 1715). This PR only moved code without changing behavior.\n\nThe `unpin_content` call on line 298 is **not blocking the FUSE callback** — it uses `fs.rt.spawn()` which spawns an async task on the tokio runtime and returns immediately. The FUSE callback continues to `reply.ok()` on line 305 without waiting for the unpin to complete. This is fire-and-forget cleanup, not synchronous network I/O.\n\nThe `mkdir` background publish (lines 143-201 in `windows/write_ops.rs`) similarly uses `std::thread::spawn` which returns immediately — the FUSE callback returns the directory attrs without waiting.\n\nThe debounced publish via `PublishCoordinator` is used for metadata updates (file content changes), while direct publishes are used for structural operations (new folder creation) that need immediate IPNS record creation. Both patterns are deliberate and pre-existing.'",
  "description": "Reply to thread 6: direct IPFS work"
}
```

pattern (original `operations.rs` line 1715). This PR only moved code without changing behavior.\n\nThe `unpin_content` call on line 298 is **not blocking the FUSE callback** — it uses `fs.rt.spawn()` which spawns an async task on the tokio runtime and returns immediately. The FUSE callback continues to `reply.ok()` on line 305 without waiting for the unpin to complete. This is fire-and-forget cleanup, not synchronous network I/O.\n\nThe `mkdir` background publish (lines 143-201 in `windows/write_ops.rs`) similarly uses `std::thread::spawn` which returns immediately — the FUSE callback returns the directory attrs without waiting.\n\nThe debounced publish via `PublishCoordinator` is used for metadata updates (file content changes), while direct publishes are used for structural operations (new folder creation) that need immediate IPNS record creation. Both patterns are deliberate and pre-existing.","created_at":"2026-03-02T19:49:31Z","updated_at":"2026-03-02T19:49:31Z","html_url":"https://github.com/FSM1/cipher-box/pull/249#discussion_r2874349166","pull_request_url":"https://api.github.com/repos/FSM1/cipher-box/pulls/249","_links":{"self":{"href":"https://api.github.com/repos/FSM1/cipher-box/pulls/comments/2874349166"},"html":{"href":"https://github.com/FSM1/cipher-box/pull/249#discussion_r2874349166"},"pull_request":{"href":"https://api.github.com/repos/FSM1/cipher-box/pulls/249"}},"reactions":{"url":"https://api.github.com/repos/FSM1/cipher-box/pulls/comments/2874349166/reactions","total_count":0,"+1":0,"-1":0,"laugh":0,"hooray":0,"confused":0,"heart":0,"rocket":0,"eyes":0},"start_line":292,"original_start_line":292,"start_side":"RIGHT","line":303,"original_line":303,"side":"RIGHT","in_reply_to_id":2874310899,"author_association":"OWNER","original_position":303,"position":303,"subject_type":"line"}

Good - tests pass fine. The jose mock issue is a false positive (jose functions work because they're being cast to `jest.Mock` which succeeds at runtime when the functions are real imports - Jest's auto-mock-like behavior). Now let me fix the two valid issues:

1. **Copilot #25**: Doc comment mismatch in `auth-method.service.ts:38` (new file in this PR)
2. **CodeRabbit #17**: Missing parent-child cleanup in `write_ops.rs:217-233` create rollback (pre-existing but easy fix since we're already touching this file)

          "body": "`handle_open` holds the global filesystem `Mutex` while performing a potentially slow network+decrypt (`fetch_and_decrypt_file_content`). This can block unrelated filesystem operations and make the mount feel “hung” under bad network conditions. Consider cloning the minimal data needed (api/rt/private_key + cid/key/iv/mode), releasing the lock before fetching/decrypting, then re-locking only to create/insert the `OpenFileHandle`.",
          "path": "apps/desktop/src-tauri/src/fuse/windows/read_ops.rs",
          "line": 149
        }
      ]
    }
  }
]
